### [68] [ZODIAC: Zero-shot Octree-based Diffusion for Anatomical Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24422)
*Miruna-Alexandra Gafencu,Vlad Bratulescu,Yordanka Velikova,Mohammad Farid Azampour,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本（zero-shot）脊柱形状补全框架，无需依赖模拟遮挡数据的监督训练，即可从术中超声的部分观测中重建完整的腰椎3D解剖结构。该方法将生成式扩散先验与自适应的八叉树结构结合，并引入"混合补全"（blended completion）机制，在推理时将解剖先验与部分观测几何融合。在体模和志愿者数据上的验证表明，该方法优于全监督基线，HD95补全误差降低22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从术中超声恢复完整的3D脊柱解剖结构是一个病态逆问题：声学遮挡和视野受限导致大量未观测区域，且视图相关的伪影使得专家标注不一致。现有监督方法依赖合成生成的不完整-完整配对数据学习条件映射，但真实术中遮挡分布与模拟分布不一致，导致泛化到患者数据时性能受限。

Method: 提出零样本形状补全框架：1) 学习完整的解剖形状的生成式扩散先验，用自适应八叉树结构表示，可在单次前向传播中高效建模完整脊柱；2) 引入混合补全机制，在推理时将学习到的解剖先验与输入的局部几何信息相融合，以应对未见过的、不规则的缺失结构模式。

Result: 在体模和志愿者数据上的实验表明，将补全与预定义的遮挡分布解耦提高了在真实遮挡条件下的泛化能力，相比全监督变体的HD95补全误差降低了22%。代码和数据已开源。

Conclusion: 将补全过程与预定义的遮挡分布解耦，能够在真实术中条件下提升脊柱形状补全的泛化性能，为解决监督方法对模拟数据分布依赖的局限提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recovering the full 3D spine anatomy from intraoperative ultrasound is an ill-posed inverse problem, as the complete structure must be inferred from incomplete and noisy observations. Acoustic occlusions and limited field of view create large unobserved regions, while view-dependent artifacts lead to variability in expert annotations of the visible anatomy. Current supervised ultrasound shape completion methods rely on synthetically generated incomplete-complete paired data to learn conditional mappings under a predefined distribution of simulated occlusions. However, real intraoperative occlusions do not necessarily follow this distribution, which can limit generalization to patient data. As a result, accurate and robust completion from noisy partial observations remains an unsolved problem. We propose a zero-shot shape completion framework that reconstructs the entire lumbar spine from partial ultrasound observations without relying on simulated training data. To accommodate unseen and irregular patterns of missing structures, we introduce blended completion, a mechanism that integrates the learned anatomical prior with incoming partial geometry at inference time. The method learns a generative diffusion prior over full anatomical shapes represented in an adaptive octree structure, enabling efficient modeling of the complete spine in a single forward pass. Validation on phantom and volunteer data shows that decoupling completion from a predefined corruption distribution improves generalisation under real occlusions, outperforming a fully supervised variant by 22% on HD95 completion error. Code and data are available at https://github.com/miruna20/ZODIAC.

</details>


### [69] [Vision Language Model Fusion for Explainable Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24430)
*Ana Estrada-Real,Lydia Alapatt,Christoph Busch,Christian Rathgeb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了多视觉语言模型（VLM）融合用于可解释人脸验证。通过让两个源VLM提供相似度分数和文本理由，第三个VLM作为决策模型，在四种融合场景下进行评估。结果表明多VLM融合不仅提升识别精度，还能提供更丰富的互补性解释，增强人脸识别系统的透明度、可审计性和错误分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 负责任的人脸验证系统不仅需要准确的决策，还需要可解释、可审计的证据，以便用户理解、评估和质疑系统的决策。虽然单一VLM结合了视觉分析和自然语言推理，但依赖单一模型可能限制决策精度和解释质量，因此需要探索多VLM融合的可能性。

Method: 本文评估了四种VLM作为独立人脸验证系统的性能，随后提出了一种融合框架：两个源模型分别提供相似度分数和文本理由，第三个VLM作为决策模型。设计了四种融合场景，逐步向决策模型提供分数、理由、人脸图像以及这些模态的组合。

Result: 多VLM融合的价值超越了识别性能本身。VLM能够提供互补的理由和视角，从而产生更丰富的人脸识别决策解释，支持更高的透明度、可审计性和错误分析。所提出的多模态VLM（结合决策分数、解释和人脸图像）在识别精度上超越了最先进的VLM和领域特定的人脸识别模型，同时提供的融合解释比单个VLM生成的解释更为稳健。

Conclusion: 多VLM融合是实现负责任的可解释人脸验证系统的有效途径，不仅提升了识别准确性，还通过互补性解释增强了系统的透明度和可审计性，有助于用户和操作人员理解最终决策及其背后的证据和潜在来源。

Abstract: Responsible deployment of face verification systems requires more than accurate decisions: systems should also provide interpretable and auditable evidence that enables users to understand, assess, and challenge their decisions. Vision-language models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation for explainable face recognition by combining visual analysis with natural-language reasoning. However, relying on a single model may further limit the decision accuracy as well as provided explanations. This work therefore investigates whether multiple VLMs can be combined to improve recognition accuracy, and to enrich the explanations associated with those decisions. This work evaluates four VLMs as standalone face verification systems and subsequently proposes a fusion framework, where two source models provide similarity scores and textual justifications and a third VLM acts as a decider model. Four different fusion scenarios are considered, progressively providing the decider model with scores, justifications, face images, and combinations of these modalities.
  Overall, the findings suggest that the value of multi-VLM fusion extends beyond recognition performance. VLMs can provide complementary justifications and perspectives that enable richer explanations of face recognition decisions, supporting greater transparency, auditability, and error analysis. This is relevant to the development of responsible explainable face verification systems, where users and operators should be able to understand not only the final decision but also the evidence and potential sources underlying it. The proposed multimodal VLM, which combines decision scores, explanations, and face images, achieves higher recognition accuracy than state-of-the-art VLMs and domain-specific face recognition models, while also providing fused explanations that are expected to be more robust than those generated by individual VLMs.

</details>


### [70] [DoublesEval: Diagnosing Multi-Agent Tactical Reasoning in Vision-Language Models via Professional Doubles Badminton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24439)
*Jintao Cheng,Weibin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DoublesEval，一个以职业双打羽毛球为测试平台的多智能体战术推理诊断评估框架，并配套提出TacticCheck测试时一致性检查方法。实验表明现有VLM在所有诊断层级上表现薄弱，TacticCheck能带来一致提升但仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）擅长描述可见场景内容，但难以推理动态多智能体交互——这类场景中动作语义依赖于协调的角色分配和时空依赖关系。现有评估框架仅衡量答案正确性，无法定位推理在何处失败，因此需要结构化的诊断性评估体系。

Method: 1) DoublesEval：以职业双打羽毛球为测试平台，采用关键时刻（key-moment）协议将回合分解为战术显著时刻，从四个可解释维度探测模型：原子识别（atomic recognition）、段内复合理解（intra-segment composite understanding）、跨段因果推理（cross-segment causal reasoning）、高层战术抽象（high-level tactical abstraction）。2) TacticCheck：轻量级、约束引导的测试时一致性检查器，利用模型自身的底层战术预测对候选答案进行重排序，推理时无需参数更新或地面真值标签。在60条精选回合（约9.6K结构化实例）上对四个代表性开源VLM进行零样本协议评估。

Result: 所有VLM在四个诊断层级上均表现薄弱，尤其以空间状态（spatial state）、交互绑定（interaction binding）和终端证据（terminal evidence）为最明显瓶颈。TacticCheck在所有评估模型上带来一致的性能提升，但距鲁棒的战术推理仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有VLM缺乏结构化的多智能体交互推理能力，需要更具互动感知、结构化评估范式的下一代VLM。DoublesEval与TacticCheck为该方向提供了诊断与改善工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Visual Language Models (VLMs) excel at describing visible scene content but struggle to reason about dynamic multi-agent interactions, where action semantics depend on coordinated roles and spatial-temporal dependencies. We formalize this capability as \textbf{multi-agent tactical reasoning} and introduce \textbf{DoublesEval}, a diagnostic evaluation framework that leverages professional doubles badminton as a structurally tractable testbed. DoublesEval employs a key-moment-based protocol that decomposes rallies into tactically salient instants and probes models across four interpretable dimensions: atomic recognition, intra-segment composite understanding, cross-segment causal reasoning, and high-level tactical abstraction. This design isolates \emph{where} reasoning fails, rather than merely measuring answer correctness. To address observed failure modes, we propose \textbf{TacticCheck}, a lightweight constraint-guided test-time consistency checker that reranks candidate answers using the model's own lower-level tactical predictions, requiring no parameter updates or ground-truth labels at inference time. Evaluating four representative open-source VLMs on 60 curated rallies (yielding $\sim$9.6K structured instances) via a zero-shot protocol, we find that models remain weak across all diagnostic levels, with especially clear bottlenecks in spatial state, interaction binding, and terminal evidence. TacticCheck delivers consistent gains across all evaluated models, while still leaving a substantial gap to robust tactical reasoning. These results highlight the need for structured, interaction-aware evaluation paradigms for next-generation VLMs. The source code is available in \href{https://github.com/Chengjt1999/DoublesEval}{\textcolor{blue}{our GitHub repository}}.

</details>


### [71] [Low-Rank Ternary Adaptation for Fine-Tuning Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24469)
*Alexandru-Dragos Manolache,Yunqiang Li,Jan van Gemert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三元乘法适配（ternary multiplicative adaptation）方法，通过低秩Kronecker分解将三元权重上的离散更新（如符号翻转或归零）表示为两个小三元矩阵的元素级乘积，在不反量化的情况下直接微调三元模型，实现参数高效且保持三元域的可合并适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元Transformer在存储和计算效率上极具优势，但现有的低比特LoRA方法无法直接微调三元权重。现有方案要么需要反量化将低比特基座权重恢复为高精度再与适配权重合并，要么只更新量化参数，导致无法获得仍保持三元域的合并模型。

Method: 提出三元乘法适配方法，将三元权重上的离散更新（如符号翻转或置零）通过低秩Kronecker分解表示为两个小三元矩阵的元素级乘积，并应用到三元权重上。该设计参数高效且表达力强，保持三元域，并支持无需反量化的直接合并。

Result: 在六个模型（涵盖语言和视觉任务）上进行了实验，包括三元化的LLaMA-3 1B和3B以及三元ViT-B/16，结果表明该方法能够恢复大部分因量化而损失的性能，并优于现有的强低比特和三元基线方法。

Conclusion: 三元乘法适配是一种有效的三元权重微调方案，既保持了参数效率和三元域约束，又支持无需反量化的直接模型合并，显著提升了量化后模型的性能恢复能力。

Abstract: Ternary transformers offer extreme memory and compute efficiency, but existing low-bit LoRA-based methods cannot directly fine-tune ternary weights. Current approaches either require dequantization, restoring low-bit base weights to higher precision to merge with adaptation weight, or update only quantization parameters, preventing a merged model that remains ternary. We propose ternary multiplicative adaptation, which represents discrete updates of ternary weights such as sign flips or zeroing through a low-rank Kronecker factorization into two small ternary matrices applied element-wise to ternary weights. This design is parameter-efficient and expressive, preserves the ternary domain, and supports direct merging without dequantization. Experiments on six models across language and vision, including ternarized LLaMA-3 1B and 3B and a ternary ViT-B/16, demonstrate that our method recovers much of the performance lost to quantization and outperforms strong low-bit and ternary baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/alexmanoo/ternary_adaptation.

</details>


### [72] [VizAnchor: Decoding Manipulation Intent from Tampering Visualizations via Dual-Anchor Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24535)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Huayuan Ye,Haiyang Zhang,Chenhui Li,Changbo Wang,Sicheng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VizAnchor是一个基于双锚点证据构建和VLM推理的可视化操纵理解框架，通过语义锚点恢复真实图表信息、空间锚点定位篡改区域，并利用三个专门智能体解码操纵方式、误导信息和误导意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据可视化安全研究仅关注定位篡改区域或恢复隐藏信息，无法解释可视化是如何被操纵的，以及这些改动为何会误导观众。缺乏对操纵机制和误导意图的深层理解。

Method: VizAnchor框架包含两个阶段：第一阶段构建语义锚点（恢复真实图表信息）和空间锚点（定位篡改区域）；第二阶段使用三个专业智能体——误导信息定位智能体（分析四面板视觉提示预测误导信息）、图表叙事重建智能体（重建原始和篡改图表的视觉叙事）、意图推断智能体（整合视觉证据推断误导意图）。同时构建了篡改定位和误导意图推断两个数据集。

Result: 实验表明VizAnchor能够准确定位操纵位置，并生成对操纵方式、误导信息和误导意图的忠实解释。

Conclusion: VizAnchor不仅能够定位篡改，还能解释为什么篡改会产生误导效果，填补了可视化操纵理解领域的空白，为数据可视化安全提供了更全面的理解框架。

Abstract: Data visualizations are widely used for communicating information, but they are also vulnerable to intentional manipulations that induce misleading interpretations. Existing methods focus on locating tampered regions or recovering hidden information, without explaining how the visualization has been manipulated or why the resulting changes may mislead viewers. We propose \textbf{VizAnchor}, a framework for visualization manipulation understanding through dual-anchor evidence construction and VLM-based reasoning. In the first stage, VizAnchor constructs a semantic anchor to recover authentic chart information and a spatial anchor to localize tampered regions. In the second stage, three specialized agents decode the manipulation. The misleader grounding agent analyzes a four-panel visual prompt to predict the misleader information. The chart narrative reconstruction agent takes the original and tampered charts as inputs and reconstructs their respective visual narratives. Finally, the intent inferring agent integrates the visual evidence and misleader information to infer the misleading intent. We further construct a dataset for tampering localization and a dataset for misleading intent inferring. Evaluation shows that VizAnchor accurately localizes manipulations and produces faithful explanations of their manipulation, misleaders, and misleading intents.

</details>


### [73] [Hierarchical Prototype-Memory Adaptation of SAM for Surgical Instrument Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24541)
*Xinning Yao,Jingjing Wang,Jinghua Yue,Xiaoyan Luo,Fugen Zhou,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HPMA框架，通过构建冻结的多尺度视觉原型记忆库并将其集成到SAM中，实现手术器械分割的层级原型-记忆适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAM手术域适配方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 纯靠分割损失优化提示或原型会使其退化为任务特定参数，而非持久的、稳定的类别记忆，导致对复杂术中变化的鲁棒性下降；(2) 多尺度视觉线索通过单一提示通路路由会造成瓶颈，阻碍有效的尺度匹配耦合。

Method: HPMA框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 从标注手术场景构建冻结的多尺度视觉原型记忆库，通过轻量适配器集成到SAM特征空间，保留稳定的类别证据；(2) 引入尺度匹配耦合机制，其中全局原型校准类别级提示特征，结构原型引导解码器对象查询，局部原型通过局部对齐目标对齐高分辨率特征图。

Result: 在公共EndoVis2017和EndoVis2018数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能，超越了现有的基础模型适配方法。

Conclusion: HPMA通过层级原型记忆适配机制有效解决了提示退化问题，并实现了多尺度线索的匹配耦合，显著提升了SAM在手术器械分割任务上的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Surgical instrument segmentation (SIS) is fundamental for computer-assisted surgery, where reliable instrument masks enable precise scene understanding and clinical assistance. Recently, adapting foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) to the surgical domain via prompt-learning has shown encouraging results. However, the performance of these adapted models under challenging surgical conditions is constrained by suboptimal adaptation mechanisms. Specifically, optimizing prompts or prototypes purely via downstream segmentation loss tends to cause them to degenerate into task-specific parameters rather than serving as persistent, stable category memory, thereby degrading their robustness against complex intraoperative variations. Moreover, routing multi-scale visual cues through a single prompt pathway creates a bottleneck that hinders effective scale-matched coupling. To address these limitations, we propose HPMA, a Hierarchical Prototype-Memory Adaptation framework for SAM. Specifically, HPMA constructs a frozen, multi-scale visual prototype memory bank from annotated surgical scenes and integrates it into SAM's feature space using lightweight adapters to preserve stable category evidence. To maximize the utility of multi-scale cues, we introduce a scale-matched coupling mechanism where global prototypes calibrate class-level prompt features, structural prototypes guide decoder object queries, and local prototypes align high-resolution feature maps through a local alignment objective. Extensive experiments on the public EndoVis2017 and EndoVis2018 datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing foundation model adaptation methods.

</details>


### [74] [KLTNet: Learning Sparse Feature Tracking for Robust and Accurate Monocular Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24544)
*Renbiao Jin,Danping Zou,Wenxian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KLTNet 是一种轻量级、即插即用的学习型稀疏特征跟踪器，用于替代传统 KLT 跟踪器，采用从粗到精、从密集到稀疏的架构，结合低分辨率稠密光流和三元组补丁精炼，并预测各向异性置信权重，在 VINS-Mono 和 OpenVINS 上显著提升跟踪精度和里程计精度，同时保持嵌入式平台的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 KLT 跟踪器主要依赖局部图像块进行特征匹配，在快速运动或低纹理环境下容易失效，从而影响基于特征的光学-惯性里程计（VIO）系统的状态估计精度与鲁棒性。

Method: KLTNet 采用从粗到精、从密集到稀疏的架构：先利用低分辨率稠密光流进行鲁棒的全局运动初始化，再通过三元组补丁精炼实现精确且时间一致的跟踪；固定参考补丁作为稳定锚点以减少跟踪漂移累积；通过可微分的多视图三角化监督学习各向异性置信权重，用作 VIO 估计器的观测权重。

Result: 在公共基准数据集和自采集的低纹理数据集上，与 VINS-Mono 和 OpenVINS 集成实验表明，KLTNet 相比传统 KLT 在特征跟踪精度和里程计精度上均有提升，并且能在嵌入式平台上保持实时性能。

Conclusion: KLTNet 作为即插即用的学习型稀疏特征跟踪器，能够有效替代经典 KLT 跟踪器，提升 VIO 系统在挑战性环境下的鲁棒性与精度，同时满足实际部署所需的实时性要求。

Abstract: Many feature-based visual-inertial odometry (VIO) systems rely on sparse feature tracking, whose accuracy and robustness directly affect state estimation. Classical KLT trackers rely primarily on local image patches and can become unreliable under rapid motion or in low-texture environments. We propose KLTNet, a lightweight learning-based, plug-and-play sparse feature tracker designed to replace classical KLT trackers in KLT-based VIO front ends. KLTNet follows a coarse-to-fine, dense-to-sparse architecture that combines low-resolution dense optical flow for robust global motion initialization with triplet-patch refinement for accurate and temporally consistent tracking. A fixed reference patch provides a stable anchor throughout each feature track and helps reduce accumulated tracking drift. In addition, KLTNet predicts anisotropic confidence weights supervised through differentiable multi-view triangulation, which can be used as observation weights in compatible VIO estimators. Experiments with VINS-Mono and OpenVINS on public benchmarks and a self-collected low-texture dataset demonstrate improved tracking and odometry accuracy over classical KLT, while maintaining real-time performance on an embedded platform.

</details>


### [75] [X-MULTI: VLM-based Imaging Factor Disentanglement for Factor-Aware Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24563)
*Sonali Godavarthy,Matthias Neuwirth-Trapp,Tim-Felix Faasch,Maarten Bieshaar,Michael Moeller,Kristof Van Laerhoven,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像生成中的成像因子解缠问题，提出两个改进：一是X-MULTI方法，利用预训练视觉语言模型监督训练中合成的未见因子组合，弥补MULTI仅基于像素级重建在观测组合上训练的不足；二是提出I-FAA指标，通过因子特定增强策略打破FAA指标中的跨因子相关性泄漏，提供更严格的解缠质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MULTI方法存在两个独立局限：(1)像素级重建目标只在观测到的成像因子组合上监督模型，对未见过的因子组合缺乏直接训练信号，限制了组合泛化能力；(2)FAA评估指标存在严重的跨因子相关性泄漏，会错误表征真实的解缠质量，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 提出X-MULTI方法，利用预训练视觉语言模型(VLM)对训练过程中合成的未见因子组合进行监督；同时提出Improved-FAA (I-FAA)指标，采用因子特定的数据增强策略来打破因子间的相关性，实现更严格的解缠评估。

Result: 实验表明X-MULTI在未见因子组合上相比MULTI实现了更好的因子对齐；同时证明FAA中的相关性泄漏会扭曲真实因子解缠的评估，I-FAA能减少这种泄漏并提供更稳健的因子对齐评估。

Conclusion: 通过VLM监督解决未见组合训练信号缺失问题，以及通过I-FAA修正评估指标的相关性泄漏，共同提升了成像因子解缠的性能和评估可靠性。

Abstract: Imaging factor disentanglement in text-to-image generation aims to independently control image acquisition properties such as types of camera lenses, sensor types, viewpoints, and domains to enable combinatorial generalization. This should let the model synthesize novel factor combinations unobserved in the training data, such as pairing a fisheye lens with an event sensor never observed in training data. Recent work, MULTI, introduced learnable, factor-specific embeddings to disentangle imaging factors, along with the Factor Alignment Accuracy (FAA) metric to evaluate disentanglement quality. We identify and address two independent limitations. First, MULTI's pixel-level reconstruction objective supervises the model only on observed imaging factor combinations, providing no direct training signal for novel combinations. We therefore propose X-MULTI, which uses a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) to supervise novel factor combinations synthesized during training. Second, we show the FAA metric exhibits severe cross-factor correlation leakage, misrepresenting true disentanglement quality. We therefore propose Improved-FAA (I-FAA), which employs factor-specific augmentation strategies to break these correlations and enables more rigorous evaluation. Experiments demonstrate that X-MULTI achieves improved factor alignment on novel combinations compared to MULTI. Moreover, we show that correlation leakage in FAA distorts the evaluation of true factor disentanglement and I-FAA reduces this leakage and therefore provides a more robust assessment of factor alignment.

</details>


### [76] [Human-Inspired Social Engagement Analysis via Interpretable Mutual Visual Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24580)
*Urwa Fatima,Mohammad Zohaib,Francesca Odone,Nicoletta Noceti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的社会互动计算模型，受心理学互视注意力理论启发，通过显式建模二元视觉注意力并将其聚合成个体和群体参与度的可解释度量，而非端到端学习互动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非语言视觉数据理解社会互动对行为分析和活动监控至关重要，但现有方法多为端到端学习，缺乏可解释性，难以被非技术用户（如教师、护理人员、社工）理解和实际使用。

Method: 构建模块化框架，结合最先进的头部朝向估计与轻量级几何推理，显式建模二元（成对）视觉注意力，并将这些线索聚合成个体和群体层面的参与度度量，输出对非技术用户友好的解释。

Result: 在多种数据上进行定量实验验证了方法的有效性，并通过定性可视化展示了其在支持教师、护理人员和社工理解群体互动动态方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的可解释、模块化的社会参与度计算框架能够有效结合头部朝向估计与几何推理，在不依赖端到端学习的条件下提供可理解的群体互动分析，具有实际应用前景。

Abstract: Understanding social interactions from non-verbal visual data is important for behavior analysis and activity monitoring. We propose an interpretable computational model of social engagement inspired by psychological theories of mutual visual attention. Rather than learning interaction patterns end-to-end, our framework explicitly models dyadic visual attention and aggregates these cues into interpretable measures of individual and group engagement. The resulting modular framework combines state-of-the-art head orientation estimation with lightweight geometric reasoning, producing explanations that remain accessible to non-technical users. We evaluate the proposed approach on a variety of data through quantitative experiments and demonstrate its practical usefulness with qualitative visualizations designed to support teachers, caregivers, and social workers in understanding group interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [77] [Comparative Assessment of Deep Learning Architectures for Underwater Subsurface Kelp Forest Segmentation with The Kelp-o-Tron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24594)
*Sundarabalan Balasubramanian,César Borja,Ana C. Murillo,Lexi N. Wilkes,Meredith L. McPherson,Kira A. Krumhansl,Jennifer A. Dijkstra,Jarrett E. K. Byrnes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种深度学习语义分割框架（ResNet34-U-Net、ResNet50-DeepLabV3及混合ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer）用于水下海带分割，基于美国东北沿海3,395对高分辨率RGB图像-掩码数据集进行评估。ResNet50-DeepLabV3（命名为Kelp-O-Tron）表现最佳，Dice为0.7120，IoU为0.6267，在分割精度、鲁棒性和泛化能力上达到最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下海带森林是重要的海岸生态系统，但实现准确的水下海带分割面临光学退化、光照变化、浊度、植被重叠及复杂底栖背景等多重挑战，亟需系统评估深度学习分割框架以支持自动化海洋生态监测。

Method: 构建了包含3,395对SSeg辅助标注的图像-掩码数据集，采用统一的预处理、数据增强和评估协议，对ResNet34-U-Net、ResNet50-DeepLabV3和混合ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer三种语义分割架构进行训练和验证，利用地理独立站点进行定量与定性评估。

Result: ResNet50-DeepLabV3在独立测试集上取得最高Dice（0.7120）和IoU（0.6267）；ResNet34-U-Net次之（Dice 0.6868；IoU 0.5978）；混合ASPP-Transformer获得最高像素精度（0.8528）但Dice（0.6437）和IoU（0.5746）较低。外部定性评估显示DeepLabV3在不同环境条件、图像质量和底栖栖息地中分割一致性更好。

Conclusion: ResNet50-DeepLabV3（Kelp-O-Tron）在分割精度、鲁棒性和泛化方面表现最佳，未来可为水下栖息地自动制图和生态监测提供有效工具；数据集和标注工作流程为相关领域研究提供了宝贵资源。

Abstract: Submerged kelp forests are vital coastal ecosystems that support marine biodiversity and ecosystem dynamics, yet accurate underwater kelp segmentation remains challenging due to optical degradation, illumination variability, turbidity, overlapping vegetation, and complex benthic backgrounds. We systematically evaluated three deep learning semantic segmentation frameworks, ResNet34-U-Net, ResNet50-DeepLabV3, and a hybrid ResNet50-ASPP-Transformer architecture, for kelp detection using high-resolution underwater RGB imagery collected from northeastern U.S. coastal waters. A dataset of 3,395 SSeg assisted annotated image-mask pairs was developed for model training and validation, while geographically independent sites were used for quantitative and qualitative evaluation. All models used consistent preprocessing, augmentation, and evaluation protocols. On independent test data, ResNet50-DeepLabV3 achieved the highest Dice (0.7120) and Intersection over Union (IoU; 0.6267), followed by ResNet34 U Net (Dice 0.6868; IoU 0.5978). The hybrid ASPP Transformer achieved the highest pixel accuracy (0.8528) but lower Dice (0.6437) and IoU (0.5746). External qualitative evaluation further showed that DeepLabV3 produced more consistent segmentation across varying environmental conditions, image qualities, and benthic habitats. Overall, ResNet50-DeepLabV3, termed Kelp-O-Tron, provided the best balance of segmentation accuracy, robustness, and generalization. The dataset, annotation workflow, and comparative evaluation provide resources for advancing automated underwater habitat mapping and ecological monitoring.

</details>


### [78] [Towards Reliable AI-Based Histological Staining: A Systematic Study of Scaling and Uncertainty in Unpaired Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24626)
*Qasim Siddiqui,Adrian Friebel,Maiju Myllys,Zaynab Hobloss,Daniela Gonzalez,Ahmed Ghallab,Stefan Hoehme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对六种无监督图像到图像翻译架构（基于GAN和基于扩散）在H&E到天狼星红（SR）虚拟染色任务上进行基准测试，覆盖54种缩放配置，并首次对无监督染色到染色架构的认知不确定性进行系统比较，发现感知质量、任务特异性误差和集成一致性三个维度基本独立，需要联合报告和选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝纤维化分期依赖天狼星红（SR）染色计算胶原蛋白比例面积（CPA），但SR染色并非所有临床中心都能开展，且相比常规H&E染色消耗更多组织、时间和试剂成本。基于AI的虚拟染色可以从H&E生成SR，但现有无监督模型的系统基准稀缺，其预测不确定性也未被量化——视觉上合理的输出未必忠实反映底层组织结构。因此需要全面基准和不确定性评估。

Method: 构建了首个公开的配对的H&E到SR小鼠肝脏数据集；对六种无监督图像到图像架构（GAN类和扩散类）在54种缩放配置下进行基准测试；每个配置从感知质量、分布对齐、任务特异性精度三个维度进行评估，并加入盲法专家阅片研究；每个家族的最佳模型再训练为深度集成，进行跨无监督染色到染色架构的认知（认知性/epistemic）不确定性系统比较。

Result: 发现感知质量、任务特异性误差和集成一致性衡量了模型适应性的基本独立轴：基于GAN的方法在感知指标上紧密聚集，但任务误差和集成一致性差异显著；基于扩散的方法（CycleDiffusion）在所有三个轴上都表现出质性不同。没有任何单一指标能捕捉这些差异。

Conclusion: 可靠的虚拟染色需要同时在感知、任务误差和不确定性三个维度上联合报告和选择模型；研究发布了数据集、切片流水线、模型和评估代码以供社区使用。

Abstract: Liver fibrosis, the principal predictor of long-term outcome in chronic liver disease, is staged from histological estimates of collagen content. Sirius Red (SR) provides the standard quantitative readout (collagen proportionate area, CPA) but is not acquired at every clinical centre and consumes tissue, time, and reagent cost beyond the routine Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) stain. AI-based virtual staining can generate SR directly from H&E, yet systematic benchmarks of unsupervised models are scarce and their predictive uncertainty has not been quantified, even though visually plausible outputs may not faithfully reproduce the underlying tissue structure. We therefore benchmark six unsupervised image-to-image architectures (GAN-based and diffusion-based) across 54 scaling configurations on a newly released paired H&E to SR mouse liver dataset, the first open resource for this translation task. Each configuration is evaluated jointly on perceptual, distributional, and task-specific axes plus a blinded expert reader study; the best per family is then retrained as a deep ensemble, the first systematic comparison of epistemic uncertainty across unsupervised stain-to-stain architectures. Across families, perceptual quality, task-specific error, and ensemble agreement measure largely independent axes of model fitness: GAN-based methods cluster tightly on perceptual metrics yet differ substantially on task error and ensemble agreement, while the diffusion-based method (CycleDiffusion) is qualitatively different on all three. No single metric captures these differences, so reliable virtual staining requires reporting and selecting on all three jointly. The dataset, tiling pipeline, models, and evaluation code are released publicly.

</details>


### [79] [On-Policy Self-Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24646)
*Wei Zhou,Xiongwei Zhu,Lingdong Kong,Bo Chen,Lei Zhang,Yongyuan Liang,Xiaoxia Hou,Ye Tian,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Linfeng Li,Shengqiong Wu,Leigang Qu,Feng Li,Wei Liu,Julian McAuley,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffusionOPSD，一种基于同策略自蒸馏的扩散模型后训练对齐框架，将图像级奖励梯度转化为对中间去噪预测的显式监督目标，在SD 3.5-M和Z-Image-Turbo上以更低训练成本取得19/20的最佳评测得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法可以用端到端奖励对齐扩散模型与人类偏好，但端点奖励无法指导中间去噪步骤如何调整，导致训练信号稀疏、缺乏可分析性，且单次更新收益难以归因。

Method: 采用双策略框架：(1) 冻结的行为策略生成轨迹并提供查询状态与锚点；(2) 奖励梯度围绕锚点构建有界的正负监督目标；(3) 可训练策略将这些目标作为分离监督进行有限步拟合；(4) 通过指数移动平均更新刷新行为策略。通过控制同一查询的实验，分别量化目标构建与有限拟合的实际收益。

Result: 在SD 3.5-M和Z-Image-Turbo两个骨干网络上、跨10个评测器、20个奖励匹配设置中取得19个最佳分数；相比最强基线提升最高44.0%；训练GPU时间相比DiffusionNFT分别减少40%（SD 3.5-M）和63%（Z-Image-Turbo）。实验还发现较大的目标构建收益不一定转化为单次拟合后的实际收益。

Conclusion: 同策略自蒸馏是将图像级奖励引导转化为显式且持续刷新的中间监督的高效且可分析的方法，为更高效、可诊断的扩散模型对齐开辟了路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific objectives, but endpoint rewards do not specify how an intermediate denoising prediction should change. We introduce DiffusionOPSD as an on-policy self-distillation framework that converts image-level reward guidance into explicit targets for clean-output predictions at sampled queries. At each outer iteration, a frozen behavior policy generates trajectories and supplies query states and anchors. Reward gradients construct bounded positive and negative targets around each anchor. The trainable policy fits these targets as detached supervision through finite fitting before an exponential moving average update refreshes the behavior policy. This setup lets us measure target construction and finite realization separately. Controlled same-query experiments show that larger target-construction gains do not necessarily translate into larger realized gains after a single fitting update. Across SD 3.5-M and the step-distilled Z-Image-Turbo, our approach achieves the best final held-out scores in 19 of 20 reward-matched settings across two backbones and ten evaluators. It outperforms the strongest competing method by up to 44.0% and reduces training GPU-hours relative to DiffusionNFT by 40% on SD 3.5-M and 63% on Z-Image-Turbo. These results support on-policy self-distillation as an efficient and analyzable approach to diffusion post-training by converting image-level reward guidance into explicit and continually refreshed intermediate supervision, thereby opening a path toward more efficient and diagnosable alignment.

</details>


### [80] [ReGround-Surg: Reliability-Guided Anchor Grounding for Referring Surgical Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24671)
*Jiaxin Wen,Ming Yin,Lu Liu,Zeyu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReGround-Surg，一个轻量级的可靠性引导锚点定位框架，通过预测文本条件的空间可靠性图，配合门控侧适配器和可靠性加权视觉到文本注意力模块，提升基于SAM2的指涉手术视频分割的初始定位精度，从而减少跟踪误差传播，在Ref-EndoVis17和Ref-EndoVis18上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM2两阶段方法（如ReSurgSAM2）的性能严重依赖初始锚定掩码的质量，一旦初始定位错误，后续跟踪会持续传播误差。手术视频中存在视觉相似的器械、遮挡和复杂的组织-器械交互，使这一问题尤为突出。

Method: ReGround-Surg首先从指涉表达式和当前帧视觉特征预测一个文本条件的空间可靠性图，然后该图被复用于两个互补分支：（1）门控侧适配器（Gated Side Adapter）在文本-视觉融合前增强与表达式相关的视觉区域；（2）可靠性加权视觉到文本注意力模块（Reliability-Weighted Vision-to-Text Attention）在提示词聚合时抑制偏离目标的视觉证据。

Result: 在Ref-EndoVis17和Ref-EndoVis18两个数据集上，跨三个评估划分均取得一致的性能提升，且速度下降可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性引导的锚点定位，能有效缓解初始掩码不佳导致的误差传播问题，为SAM2框架下的指涉手术视频分割提供了轻量且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Referring surgical video segmentation requires segmenting a target instrument or tissue region across video frames according to a natural language expression. Recent Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) based two-stage methods (e.g., ReSurgSAM2) first ground the referred target in an initial or selected frame, then propagate the selected mask via tracking. Although effective, their performance is highly sensitive to the quality of the initial grounded mask: once an incorrect anchor is selected, subsequent tracking tends to propagate the error. This issue is especially challenging in surgical videos due to visually similar instruments, occlusion, and complex tissue-tool interactions. To address this issue, we propose ReGround-Surg, a lightweight reliability-guided anchor grounding framework to improve SAM2-based referring surgical video segmentation. It first predicts a text-conditioned spatial reliability map from the referring expression and current-frame visual features. The map is then reused in two complementary branches: a Gated Side Adapter enhances expression-relevant visual regions before text-to-vision fusion, while a Reliability-Weighted Vision-to-Text Attention module suppresses off-target visual evidence during prompt-token aggregation. Experiments on Ref-EndoVis17 and Ref-EndoVis18 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods across three evaluation splits with negligible speed reduction. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/JiaxinWen1/ReGround-Surg.

</details>


### [81] [TurboT2VA: Fast Large-Scale Text-to-Video-Audio Generation via Score-Regularized Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24674)
*Xiaoda Yang,Yuxiang Liu,Kaiwen Zheng,Yuan Liu,Yibo Lai,Shengpeng Ji,Kai Jiang,Jianfei Chen,Xiaobin Hu,Shuicheng Yan,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboT2VA是一个用于加速19B参数联合文本到视频-音频生成模型的蒸馏与推理框架，通过渐进式蒸馏课程（离散一致性预热、连续一致性精炼、联合一致性-分布匹配）和架构感知的推理栈，在标准分辨率下实现20.1倍加速、在高分辨率部署下实现54.67倍加速，同时保持生成质量与视频-音频同步性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合文本到视频-音频（T2VA）生成能产生同步的视觉和音频内容，但大型模型的长时间采样轨迹和异构多模态计算使得推理成本极其昂贵，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: TurboT2VA包含两个核心部分：(1) 蒸馏框架：通过逐模态归一化解决模态不均衡优化问题，采用渐进式课程包含离散一致性预热、连续一致性精炼和联合一致性-分布匹配三个阶段，先建立稳定多样的生成轨迹再引入分布级精炼；(2) 推理栈：结合guard W8A8量化和融合算子、填充文本压缩、模态感知稀疏注意力，同时保留密集的跨模态和文本条件路径。

Result: 在LTX-2上，四步蒸馏将生成器延迟从50.52秒降至2.51秒（512×768分辨率下20.1倍加速），同时保持强视觉质量、音频保真度、多样性和视频-音频同步性；在1024×1792高分辨率部署下，完整推理栈将生成器延迟从318.74秒降至5.83秒（单NVIDIA H20上54.67倍加速）。

Conclusion: TurboT2VA证明通过精心设计的渐进式蒸馏课程和架构感知推理优化，可以在大幅加速大规模多模态生成的同時保持生成质量和跨模态同步性，为实际部署联合视频-音频模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Joint text-to-video-audio generation produces synchronized visual and acoustic content, but the long sampling trajectories and heterogeneous multimodal computation of large models make inference prohibitively expensive. We present TurboT2VA, a distillation and inference framework for accelerating a 19B-parameter joint video-audio model. Large-scale T2VA distillation is challenged by modality-imbalanced optimization, the difficulty of continuous-time consistency training at scale, and the quality--diversity trade-off. TurboT2VA addresses these issues with per-modality normalization and a progressive curriculum comprising discrete consistency warm-up, continuous consistency refinement, and joint consistency--distribution matching. The curriculum first establishes a stable, diverse generation trajectory and only then introduces distribution-level refinement. On LTX-2, four-step distillation reduces generator latency from 50.52s to 2.51s at the standard evaluation resolution of 512$\times$768, achieving a 20.1$\times$ speedup while maintaining strong visual quality, audio fidelity, diversity, and video-audio synchronization. We further develop an architecture-aware inference stack that combines guarded W8A8 and fused operators, padded-text compaction, and modality-aware sparse attention while preserving dense cross-modal and text-conditioning paths. Under the high-resolution deployment setting at 1024$\times$1792, the complete stack reduces generator latency from 318.74s to 5.83s on one NVIDIA H20, achieving a 54.67$\times$ generator-only speedup. Inference code and generation demos are available at https://github.com/thu-ml/TurboDiffusion/tree/main/turbot2va.

</details>


### [82] [Game2World Engine: Unlocking In-the-Wild Gameplay Videos for World Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24680)
*Wenxuan Shen,Dongna Jin,Dongping Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Game2World框架，系统解决游戏视频中UI干扰世界模型训练的问题。该框架包含GameUI-Taxonomy（UI分类体系）和G2WEngine（UI提取与合成引擎），构建了96K合成配对视频和1,079个野外片段的游戏世界数据集。基于此提出GameCleaner，一种无掩码的游戏UI去除模型，能直接识别并移除多样HUD元素并保留底层场景。实验表明UI去除后的视频训练的世界模型VideoReward提升6.83%，GameCleaner在合成视频上AAR达95.36，比最强的时序掩码基线高57.3%，野外AAR为80.05且背景保留率达99.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏视频是视频世界模型的规模化训练数据来源，但原始游戏视频中，游戏世界与屏幕空间界面（UI/HUD）纠缠在一起，引入游戏特定的偏差和不相关的动态，阻碍了世界模型的训练效果。

Method: 提出GameUI-Taxonomy（形式化游戏UI接地与移除的分类体系）和G2WEngine（全栈框架，从真实游戏视频中自动提取可复用UI资产，并在干净视频上合成时序连贯的UI叠加层）。基于此构建Game2World数据集（96K合成配对视频+1,079个野外片段+5,132个验证过的UI元素覆盖21个分类类别）。进一步提出GameCleaner，一种无掩码游戏UI去除模型，结合多模态语义理解与视频编辑能力，直接识别并移除HUD元素同时保留场景内容和时序动态。

Result: 在受控实验中，基于无UI游戏视频训练的世界模型在VideoReward上比基于含UI视频训练的模型提升6.83%。在UI去除评估中，GameCleaner在合成视频上平均AAR达95.36，比最强时序掩码基线高出57.3%；在野外视频上获得最佳AAR 80.05，背景保留率达99.8%。

Conclusion: 实验证明将互联网游戏视频转化为高质量世界模型训练数据具有规模化潜力。代码、数据集和模型将公开发布。

Abstract: Video games provide a scalable source of training data for video world models, offering diverse environments, complex interactions, and abundant in-the-wild gameplay videos. However, raw gameplay footage entangles the game world with screen-space interfaces, introducing game-specific biases and irrelevant dynamics that hinder world-model training. To address this problem, we introduce GameUI-Taxonomy and G2WEngine, a full-stack framework that formalizes gameplay UI grounding and removal. G2WEngine automatically extracts reusable UI assets from real gameplay videos and synthesizes temporally coherent UI overlays on clean footage. Using this engine, we construct Game2World, comprising 96K synthetic paired videos with precise reconstruction targets and 1,079 in-the-wild clips from 303 games for realistic evaluation. Its asset library contains 5,132 verified UI elements across 21 taxonomy categories, collected from 1,010 representative gameplay frames. Based on Game2World, we propose GameCleaner, a mask-free gameplay UI removal model that combines multimodal semantic understanding with video editing capabilities. Unlike mask-based methods, GameCleaner directly identifies and removes diverse HUD elements while preserving the underlying scene content and temporal dynamics. In a controlled pilot, world models trained on UI-free gameplay improve overall VideoReward by 6.83% over those trained on UI-overlaid data. On UI-removal evaluation, GameCleaner achieves an average AAR of 95.36 on synthetic videos, outperforming the strongest temporal mask baseline by 57.3%, and obtains the best in-the-wild AAR of 80.05 with 99.8 background preservation. These results demonstrate the scalable potential of transforming Internet gameplay videos into high-quality world-model training data. Code, dataset, and model will be available at https://github.com/Dongping-Chen/Game2World.

</details>


### [83] [Deep Learning Super Resolution for Satellite Cloud Mask Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24715)
*Angelos Georgakis,Valentina Kanaki,Giorgos Giannopoulos,Stella Girtsou,Ioannis Kontogiorgakis,Charalampos Kontoes,Kostas Philippopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于深度学习的超分辨率方法（SpatialCNN和SpatialGAN）用于对SEVIRI云掩膜产品进行4倍空间下采样增强，并创建了跨传感器的SEVMOD-CM云掩膜数据集，该数据集通过对MODIS和SEVIRI卫星观测进行时空匹配构建。实验结果表明，所提方法优于双三次插值基线，为遥感领域提供高质量的云掩膜增强方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过一半的光学卫星数据受雾霾或云层影响，且卫星数据面临空间与时间分辨率之间的根本性权衡，难以获取连续的高分辨率云观测数据。这一挑战限制了大气监测、天气预报等应用的发展。

Method: 构建了一个跨传感器云掩膜数据集SEVMOD-CM，通过对MODIS和SEVIRI卫星观测进行空间和时间匹配创建。提出了两种深度学习方法：基于CNN的SpatialCNN和基于GAN的SpatialGAN，利用SEVIRI光谱和云掩膜产品输入，预测对应的MODIS云掩膜，实现4倍跨传感器域的空间增强。

Result: 两种方法均通过实验评估，并与标准双三次插值上采样技术进行对比。实验结果证明了所提模型和数据集对遥感社区的价值，展示了超分辨率技术应用于地球静止卫星云掩膜产品的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习方法能够有效实现卫星云掩膜产品的4倍空间超分辨率增强，对大气监测、天气预报、灾害风险降低、太阳能预测和气候研究等应用具有显著价值。

Abstract: A vast amount of optical satellite data is being transmitted to Earth-based servers every day, and more than half of this data is affected by haze or clouds. Additionally, this data suffers from the fundamental trade-off between spatial and temporal resolution, which remains largely unresolved, making the acquisition of continuous high-resolution satellite observations of clouds an ongoing challenge. This work addresses this challenge by proposing two Deep Learning super-resolution methods for the accurate downscaling of SEVIRI cloud mask products, as well as a novel cross-sensor cloud mask dataset called SEVMOD-CM, created by spatially and temporally matching MODIS and SEVIRI satellite observations. The two proposed models are a CNN-based (SpatialCNN) and a GAN-based (SpatialGAN) Neural Network. Trained on the SEVIRI spectral and cloud mask products, the proposed methods predict the corresponding MODIS Cloud masks, achieving a 4x spatial enhancement across sensor domains. Both approaches are evaluated experimentally, and compared against the standard bicubic interpolation upsampling technique. The experimental results demonstrate the value of the proposed models and dataset for the remote sensing community, highlighting the benefits of applying super-resolution techniques to geostationary-derived cloud mask products for applications such as atmospheric monitoring, weather forecasting, disaster risk reduction, solar energy forecasting, and climate research.

</details>


### [84] [Interpretable Fundus Image Classification via Ring-Based Retinal Vasculature Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24723)
*Xiaoyan Li,Shixin Xu,Arvind Gupta,Huaxiong Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于环状视网膜血管结构的可解释眼底图像分类框架，仅使用定量血管描述符（几何、颜色、氧合相关外观、熵）即可在三个公开数据集上取得强分类性能，在HRF上达到91.1%准确率，与大规模预训练的视觉Transformer模型RETFound相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代眼底图像分类管线依赖深度潜在表征，可解释性有限；临床筛查和生物标志物发现需要可解释、可量化的分析方法。

Method: 围绕视盘的同心环状区域提取共四种生理驱动的血管描述符：血管几何、颜色外观、与氧合相关的血管外观，以及血管与背景之间的熵，从血管掩膜、图像强度和光密度测量中导出并跨环聚合。

Result: 仅用定量血管描述符即取得强分类性能；在HRF上达91.1%准确率（使用自动生成血管掩膜），与使用相同评估设置的RETFound（大规模眼底图像预训练ViT）相当。额外分析显示预训练图像模型对采集相关的空间线索（眼底尺度、视野内位置）及非血管图像特征敏感。

Conclusion: 该框架有望支持可解释的疾病分类、定量视网膜表型分析和视网膜生物标志物发现，且无需大量任务特定训练数据。

Abstract: Retinal fundus photography is widely used for screening and monitoring ocular diseases, but many modern classification pipelines rely on deep latent representations and provide limited interpretability. This study develops an interpretable fundus image classification framework based on a ring-structured representation of the retinal vasculature centered on the optic disc. The method quantifies vessel geometry, color appearance, oxygenation-related vascular appearance, and vessel--background entropy within concentric retinal regions. These physiologically motivated descriptors are derived from vessel masks, image intensities, and optical-density measurements and aggregated across rings to capture spatial variation in vascular properties. Using only quantitative vascular descriptors, the proposed method achieved strong classification performance across three public fundus datasets. On HRF, it achieved 91.1\% accuracy using automatically generated vessel masks, matching RETFound, a vision transformer pretrained on large-scale retinal fundus image data, under the same evaluation setting. Additional analyses suggest that pretrained image models are sensitive to acquisition-related spatial cues, including fundus scale and retinal position within the field of view, as well as broader non-vessel image characteristics. This framework may support interpretable disease classification, quantitative retinal phenotyping, and retinal biomarker discovery without requiring large task-specific training datasets.

</details>


### [85] [TorchMorph: CUDA-accelerated Morphological Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24738)
*Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍 TorchMorph，一个轻量级 PyTorch 扩展，将 scipy.ndimage 的形态学变换算子（二值/灰度形态学、距离变换、Sinkhorn 最优传输）以融合 CUDA 内核实现，直接操作 (B, C, Spatial...) 张量，支持最多 8 个空间维度，API 逐参数对齐 scipy.ndimage。相比 CPU 单线程参考实现，批量执行在灰度形态学上可达 1100 倍吞吐，精确欧氏距离变换 350 倍，Sinkhorn 比 POT 快 42 倍，且在精度上严格对齐 SciPy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scipy.ndimage 是 Python 生态中形态学变换的事实标准参考实现，但它是 CPU-only、单数组的，无法在 GPU 训练循环中直接使用，因为需要昂贵的设备到主机往返传输。现有的基于 PyTorch 的 GPU 视觉库仅覆盖少量算子，通常限制在二维空间和扁平结构元素。这形成了一个功能覆盖空白：GPU 上缺乏与 SciPy 完全兼容的形态学算子集合。

Method: TorchMorph 采用分层架构实现，包含 22 个公开算子，覆盖二值形态学、灰度形态学、精确与近似距离变换、熵正则化最优传输四类算子族。所有算子以融合 CUDA 内核实现，直接操作 (B, C, Spatial...) 格式的 CUDA 张量，支持最多八个空间维度。API 刻意逐参数镜像 scipy.ndimage，包括边界模式、结构元素原点与预分配输出，使得现有代码只需更换 import 即可迁移。

Result: 与单线程 CPU 参考相比，批量执行在灰度形态学上达到 1.1e3 倍吞吐量，精确欧氏距离变换达 350 倍，Sinkhorn 求解器比 POT 快 42 倍。二值和 chamfer 算子在数值上精确复现 SciPy 对应实现，所有浮点算子与 CPU 参考的绝对误差在 1.8e-6 以内。

Conclusion: TorchMorph 填补了 PyTorch GPU 生态中形态学算子的功能空白，提供了与 SciPy 参数级兼容的 GPU 实现，兼具高性能与数值精度，以 MIT 许可证开源发布。

Abstract: Morphological transforms are long-standing tools for shape and mask processing, but the de facto reference implementation in the Python ecosystem, i.e. scipy.ndimage, is CPU-only, single-array, and therefore unusable inside a GPU training loop without an expensive device-to-host round trip. GPU vision libraries built on PyTorch cover a narrow subset of these operators, typically restricted to two spatial dimensions and flat structuring elements. We present TorchMorph, a lightweight PyTorch extension that closes this gap. TorchMorph exposes 22 public operators covering binary morphology, greyscale morphology, exact and approximate distance transforms, and entropy-regularised optimal transport, all implemented as fused CUDA kernels that operate directly on (B, C, Spatial...) CUDA tensors with up to eight spatial dimensions. The API deliberately mirrors scipy.ndimage argument-for-argument, including border modes, structuring-element origins and pre-allocated outputs, so that existing pipelines port with a change of import. We describe the layered architecture and the kernel designs behind each operator family. Against single-threaded CPU references, batched execution reaches up to 1.1e3 times the throughput of scipy.ndimage on greyscale morphology and up to 350x on exact Euclidean distance transforms, while the Sinkhorn solver runs up to 42x faster than POT. Binary and chamfer operators reproduce their SciPy counterparts exactly, and every float-valued operator agrees with the CPU reference to within 1.8e-6 absolute error. TorchMorph is released under the MIT licence at https://intcomp.github.io/tm.

</details>


### [86] [Weakly Supervised Seafloor Segmentation for Seagrass Habitat Mapping in Side-Scan Sonar Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24756)
*Hayat Rajani,Nuno Gracias,Rafael Garcia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于弱监督语义分割的框架，利用侧扫声纳（SSS）影像仅通过图像级标签实现海岸带海草床底栖栖息地的像素级制图，在测试横断面上达到87.6%的mIoU，解决了传统光学遥感无法覆盖深水/浑浊水域、而SSS人工标注成本高昂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海草床是重要的蓝色碳汇栖息地，精确测绘其范围是海岸管理和碳核算的前提。光学卫星遥感无法到达深水或浑浊水域，而侧扫声纳能以高分辨率在任何深度成像海底；但SSS影像的解译仍依赖密集的人工标注，过程缓慢且成本高昂，亟需一种标注高效的自动化方法。

Method: 该框架采用弱监督语义分割方法，仅使用图像级标签训练像素级分割模型。具体包含：(1) ViT编码器-解码器与分类分支耦合，提取类激活图(CAM)；(2) 利用针对声学影像噪声和弱边界优化调参的密集条件随机场(dense CRF)将CAM细化为伪标签；(3) 迭代自训练方案配合采样策略以应对数据严重的类别不平衡；(4) 对比多种损失函数，发现Lovász-Softmax损失效果最佳；(5) 在无标注SSS数据上进行自监督预训练以进一步提升性能。

Result: 在留出横断面上，细化后的伪标签相对真实标注达到89.3%的mIoU；未使用任何像素级标签训练的分割分支达到87.6%的mIoU。自监督预训练进一步提升了3%的mIoU。野外试验验证了训练模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，利用侧扫声纳进行准确且标注高效的海底栖息地测绘是可行的，能够满足海岸尺度海草床监测的规模化需求，为蓝碳核算和沿海管理提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Seagrass meadows are crucial blue-carbon habitats, and mapping their extent is a prerequisite for coastal management and carbon inventory. Optical satellite sensors cover large areas but cannot reach deep or turbid water, whereas side-scan sonar (SSS) images the seabed at high resolution and at any depth. Interpreting SSS, however, still relies on dense manual annotation, which is slow and costly. We address this by adapting a weakly supervised semantic segmentation framework to SSS benthic habitat mapping, so that pixel-level maps are learned from image-level labels alone. The framework couples a ViT-based encoder-decoder with a classification branch, extracts class activation maps, and refines them into pseudo-labels with a dense conditional random field that we tune for the noise and weak boundaries of acoustic imagery. It follows an iterative self-training scheme, together with a sampling strategy to cope with the strong class imbalance of the data. We also study the effect of different loss functions on segmentation quality, finding Lovász-Softmax loss the most effective. On a held-out transect, the refined pseudo-labels reached an mIoU of 89.3\% against the ground truth, and the segmentation branch, trained without any pixel-level labels, reached 87.6\%. Self-supervised pretraining on unlabelled SSS added a further 3\% in mean intersection-over-union. Field trials further demonstrate the generalizability of the trained model. These results show that accurate and label-efficient benthic habitat mapping from side-scan sonar is feasible at the scale needed for coast-wide seagrass monitoring.

</details>


### [87] [IDeaL: Data-Free Multi-Teacher Distillation via Improved Dead Leaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24759)
*Feyza Yavuz,Mert Bülent Sarıyıldız,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多教师蒸馏的无数据样本生成方法 IDeaL，通过为不同教师定制噪声（结合图像级和图像块级的去相关损失），生成教师特化且改善的样本，使学生在无真实训练数据的情况下也能捕捉多个教师的互补信息，实验结果显著缩小了与使用真实图像蒸馏的学生之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师蒸馏通常假设可以访问教师训练数据的并集，但该假设在实际中往往不成立。作者质疑这一假设，探索无数据场景下如何利用多个教师模型进行知识蒸馏，从而摆脱对真实训练数据的依赖。

Method: 首先研究从输入不同类型噪声的教师进行蒸馏所能达到的效果；进而提出利用教师中包含的信息来定制噪声的方法：通过patch级和图像级的去相关损失，为每个教师生成特化的改进样本，优化无数据蒸馏过程。最终方法命名为IDeaL（Improved samples for data-free multi-teacher distillation）。

Result: 实验表明，使用IDeaL生成的最有效样本训练出的学生模型成功捕捉了教师的互补信息，取得了令人惊讶的竞争性结果，大幅缩小了与使用真实图像蒸馏的学生之间的差距。在仅使用1K图像进行蒸馏的预算下，基于IDeaL蒸馏的学生性能匹配甚至超越了使用ImageNet 1K图像子集蒸馏的学生。

Conclusion: 无数据多教师蒸馏是可行且高效的。通过为每个教师定制噪声生成样本，可以在不访问真实训练数据的情况下，有效聚合多个教师的互补知识，达到接近甚至媲美使用真实数据蒸馏的效果。

Abstract: Multi-teacher distillation has emerged as a way to combine complementary teacher models into a single student model that exhibits the strengths of all its teachers. The student is trained to mimic the output of the teachers on a set of images, typically the union of the individual teacher's training sets, assuming this data is available. In this paper, we question that assumption and explore alternative options. We first study how far one can go when distilling from teachers fed with different types of noise. Then, we show that information contained in the teachers can be leveraged to tailor the noise for multi-teacher distillation: we propose a method that, thanks to decorrelation losses at both patch and image levels, generates teacher-specific, improved samples optimized for data-free distillation. Experiments show that our most effective samples, IDeaL, lead to strong students that successfully capture complementary information from the teachers, yielding surprisingly competitive results that substantially narrow the gap with students distilled from real images. Moreover, given a limited budget of 1K images for distillation, students distilled using our IDeaL samples match or surpass the performance of those distilled using a 1K-image subset of ImageNet.

</details>


### [88] [MoTE: Mixture of Task Experts for Multi-Task Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24763)
*Muhammad Asad Ali,Umar Khan,Nadia Robertini,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoTE（Mixture of Task Experts）是一种将大语言模型前馈网络转化为任务特定专家的解码器架构，每个样本仅遵循一条任务级路由，在COIN基准上以约2B参数激活量超越了现有VideoLLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程式视频-语言模型需要从相同视觉证据解决异构任务（动作识别、预测、过程预测）。稠密Transformer解码器在不同任务间共享前馈网络，导致任务行为纠缠且难以可控扩展；而稀疏MoE解码器的token级学习路由与任务级过程目标不自然对齐。

Method: MoTE将LLM前馈网络转换为任务特定专家，同时保持多模态骨干网络共享。每个样本遵循一条样本级任务路由，使得激活的任务专家计算量独立于存储的专家总数。该设计实例化为VideoLLM-MoTE，在五个COIN基准上使用显式任务路由进行评测。

Result: 五个专家的模型每样本激活约20亿LLM参数，在平均top-1准确率上超越近期VideoLLM基线。在相同专家拓扑下，优于稠密全专家激活方案和学习式稀疏路由控制方案。

Conclusion: 任务结构化路由为多任务视频-语言学习提供了一种可解释、计算高效且支持可控能力扩展的解码器替代方案。

Abstract: Procedural video-language models must solve heterogeneous tasks from the same visual evidence, including action recognition, forecasting, and procedure prediction. Dense transformer decoders share the same feed-forward networks across tasks, which can entangle task behavior and make controlled capability expansion difficult. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoders provide conditional computation, but token-level learned routing is not naturally aligned with task-level procedural objectives. We propose MoTE (Mixture of Task Experts), a decoder architecture that converts large language model feed-forward networks into task-specific experts while keeping the multimodal backbone shared. Each example follows one sample-level task route, so active task-expert computation remains independent of the number of stored task experts. We instantiate this design as VideoLLM-MoTE and evaluate it on five COIN benchmarks using explicit task routes. The five-expert model activates ~2B LLM parameters per sample and achieves higher average top-1 accuracy than recent VideoLLM baselines. Under the same expert topology, it improves over dense all-expert activation and learned sparse-routing controls. These results show that task-structured routing provides an interpretable and compute-efficient decoder alternative for multi-task video-language learning.

</details>


### [89] [Ensemble of Convolutional Neural Networks for StrokePrediction: Towards Improved Diagnostic Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24771)
*Md Shahriar Sajid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于11个特征的智能中风预测系统，通过7种监督学习算法进行评估，发现集成方法（随机森林、堆叠分类器、Bagging分类器）准确率最高达99.52%，验证了集成方法在中风分类中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中具有高死亡率和高发病率，快速干预对生存至关重要。早期诊断和预防措施能显著减少生命损失和残疾，因此需要开发基于深度学习的新型计算机辅助诊断技术来实现中风的早期检测。

Method: 使用11个特征构建预测系统，通过文献综述、数据集可视化、数据预处理和模型评估流程，对7种监督机器学习算法（随机森林、堆叠分类器、Bagging分类器、决策树、KNN、TabNet、自定义前馈网络、SVC、逻辑回归）进行比较评估。

Result: 随机森林、堆叠分类器和Bagging分类器达到最高准确率99.52%；决策树准确率达98.24%；KNN和TabNet分别为96.73%和96.49%；自定义前馈模型为94.91%；SVC和逻辑回归较低，分别为88.06%和77.03%。

Conclusion: 集成方法在中风分类任务中表现出显著优势，验证了其在脑卒中预测中的有效性，为早期诊断提供了可靠的智能辅助手段。

Abstract: Brain stroke, known for its high mortality and incidence rates, poses significant health risks and requires rapid intervention for survival. Early diagnosis and preventive measures can greatly reduce life loss and disabilities. Recent advancements in deep learning have led to novel computer-aided diagnostic techniques for early stroke detection. This study proposes an intelligent system that predicts potential strokes using eleven features, evaluated through seven supervised machine learning algorithms. The process includes a literature review, dataset visualization, data preprocessing, and model evaluation. Ensemble methods like Random Forest, Stacking Classifier, and Bagging Classifier achieved high accuracies of 99.52%, while Decision Tree reached 98.24%. Other models, including KNN and TabNet, demonstrated reliable performance, achieving accuracies of 96.73% and 96.49%, respectively. The custom feedforward model achieved 94.91%, while SVC and logistic regression had lower accuracies at 88.06% and 77.03%. The results highlight the effectiveness of ensemble methods in stroke classification.

</details>


### [90] [Image Difference Quantification Using Autoencoder-Based Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24782)
*Manish Sharma,Timothy Yim,Clifton Forlines*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于卷积自编码器的图像相似度评估框架，通过隐空间中的余弦相似度来衡量图像差异，替代传统MSE、PSNR、SSIM等像素级指标，在dog-cat图像及多个跨域数据集上展现出强类别可分性，并在TID2013数据集上验证了与主观感知评分的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像相似度指标（MSE、PSNR、SSIM）基于像素级比较，无法捕捉感知上有意义的图像差异；而深度神经网络学习的隐层表征包含与人类视觉感知更为一致的高层语义信息，因此需要一种基于语义表征的图像相似度度量方法。

Method: 构建卷积自编码器框架，将图像映射到紧凑的隐空间嵌入向量，通过计算隐空间中两个向量的余弦相似度来量化图像差异。该方法利用自编码器学到的语义特征，对光照、姿态和背景变化具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在dog-cat图像及跨域数据集上，隐空间呈现清晰的类别聚类和强类间可分性，98.4%的dog-cat图像对相似度分数低于0.5；在TID2013数据集上，隐空间距离与人类平均意见评分（MOS）呈正相关，表明对感知相关失真敏感。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法提供了计算高效且语义可解释的替代方案，优于传统像素级相似度指标，可用于基于内容的图像检索、感知质量评估和语义相似性分析等应用。

Abstract: Traditional image similarity metrics such as Mean Squared Error (MSE), Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), and the Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) rely on pixel-level comparisons and often fail to capture perceptually meaningful differences between images. In contrast, latent representations learned by deep neural networks encode high-level semantic information that is more closely aligned with human visual perception. This paper proposes a convolutional autoencoder-based framework for quantifying image differences using cosine similarity in latent space. The learned compact embeddings enable robust differentiation between visually distinct images under variations in illumination, pose, and background. Extensive evaluation on dog-cat images and additional cross-domain datasets demonstrates clear class-wise clustering and strong inter-class separability in the latent space, with 98.4% of dog-cat image pairs exhibiting similarity scores below 0.5. Further validation using the TID2013 dataset shows that latent-space distance correlates positively with human Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), demonstrating sensitivity to perceptually relevant image distortions. The proposed approach provides a computationally efficient and semantically grounded alternative to conventional pixel-based similarity metrics, with potential applications in content-based retrieval, perceptual quality assessment, and semantic similarity analysis.

</details>


### [91] [MoE-based Feature Adapter for Prompt-free Binary Coronary Artery Segmentation in X-ray Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24783)
*Lin Xi,Yingliang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于提示无关混合专家（MoE）特征适配器的冠状动脉X射线血管造影视频分割方法，通过多个轻量级专家和输入依赖的top-k路由机制，自适应精细化血管相关特征，在MOSXAV和XACV数据集上优于多个代表性基线并提升跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉在X射线血管造影中细小且对比度低，导管、导丝和复杂解剖背景会干扰血管分割。现有U-Net和Transformer模型提供强基线，但其共享的特征适配通路难以应对异构的血管造影外观表现，需要更灵活的自适应特征处理方法。

Method: 基于参数高效Vision Transformer适配器，提出一种无需提示（prompt-free）的MoE特征适配器。采用多个轻量级专家模块，并通过输入依赖的top-k路由机制动态选择专家来细化血管相关特征，同时限制活跃计算成本。

Result: 在MOSXAV数据集上的实验以及XACV数据集上的外部评估表明，所提出的方法优于代表性基线模型，并显著改善了跨数据集的泛化性能。

Conclusion: MoE-based适配器学习对于X射线血管造影视频中的鲁棒冠状动脉分割是有效的，为异构医疗影像特征自适应提供了一种可行的新思路。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of coronary arteries in X-ray angiography videos is essential for quantitative coronary analysis and image-guided interventions. However, accurate segmentation remains challenging because coronary vessels are thin and exhibit low contrast, while the presence of catheters, guidewires, and complex anatomical background structures can further interfere with vessel delineation. Existing U-Net- and Transformer-based models provide strong baselines, but their shared feature-adaptation pathways may be insufficient for heterogeneous angiographic appearances. In this paper, we propose a prompt-free mixture-of-experts (MoE) feature adapter for binary coronary artery segmentation. Built upon parameter-efficient Vision Transformer adapters, the proposed method uses multiple lightweight experts with input-dependent top-$k$ routing to adaptively refine vessel-related features while limiting active computational cost. Experiments on MOSXAV and external evaluation on XACV show that the proposed method outperforms representative baselines and improves cross-dataset generalisation. These results suggest that MoE-based adapter learning is effective for robust coronary artery segmentation in X-ray angiography videos.

</details>


### [92] [EMFE: A lightweight, explainable machine learning framework for malaria cell classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24793)
*Md Abdullah Al Kafi,Walayat Hussain,Mousumi Karmakar,Sumit Kumar Banshal,Ahmed Al Marouf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EMFE框架，一种仅用五个特征的轻量级疟疾诊断方法，通过颜色归一化、阈值分割和经典机器学习达到94.6%的准确率，与深度学习方法相当但计算成本极低且可解释性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度卷积神经网络的自动化疟疾诊断虽然准确但存在三大缺陷：计算成本高昂、可解释性差、缺乏患者级别的严格验证。本文旨在提供一种轻量级、可解释且统计严谨的替代方案。

Method: EMFE框架包含五个特征，流程为：Gray World颜色归一化、自适应绿色通道阈值化、形态学斑点检测、经典机器学习分类。使用NIH LHNCBC数据集（27,558张图像，200名患者），采用患者分组的嵌套交叉验证（外层20折、内层3折），比较随机森林、直方图梯度提升和支持向量机三种分类器。

Result: 优化后的随机森林达到94.6%的池化折外准确率（95% CI [93.6, 95.7]），在40名未接触患者的保留测试集上准确率为94.3%，患者级别置换检验p<0.001（1,000次置换）。消融实验量化了各特征贡献，点饱和度被识别为最主要的判别特征。

Conclusion: EMFE证明了在统计严谨、可解释且计算轻量的条件下，经典方法可以达到与深度学习相当的诊断性能，同时明确量化了自身的局限性（包括三种失败模式和误报累积效应）。

Abstract: Automated malaria diagnosis from stained blood-smear microscopy is dominated by deep convolutional neural networks that are accurate but computationally expensive, poorly interpretable, and rarely validated with patient-level rigor. We present EMFE (Efficient Mathematical Feature Extraction), a five-feature framework for classifying single red-blood-cell images as parasitized or uninfected using Gray World color normalization, adaptive green-channel thresholding, morphological spot detection, and classical machine learning. Using the NIH LHNCBC malaria dataset (27,558 images from 200 patients), we evaluate Random Forest, Histogram Gradient Boosting, and Support Vector Machine classifiers under patient-grouped nested cross-validation (K_outer=20, K_inner=3), ensuring that cells from each patient remain within a single fold. The optimized Random Forest achieves 94.6% pooled out-of-fold accuracy (95% CI [93.6, 95.7]), corroborated by an untouched 40-patient holdout test (94.3%) and a patient-level permutation test (p<0.001, 1,000 permutations). Ablation experiments quantify the contribution of individual features and pipeline stages. Hardware-matched comparisons with retrained DenseNet121, ResNet50, and MobileNetV2 models assess the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. Synthetic perturbations characterize three failure modes, while explainability analysis identifies spot saturation as the dominant discriminative feature. Patient-level aggregation further quantifies sensitivity-specificity trade-offs and false-positive accumulation. These results demonstrate a statistically rigorous, interpretable, and computationally lightweight alternative to deep learning, while explicitly quantifying its limitations.

</details>


### [93] [LAION-BVD: A 10-Million-Hour Open Video Dataset for Multimodal Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24845)
*Andreas Hochlehnert,Marianna Nezhurina,Mehdi Cherti,Andrej Radonjic,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Christoph Schuhmann,Romain Beaumont,Wieland Brendel,Bernhard Schölkopf,A. Sophia Koepke,Jenia Jitsev,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAION-BVD是一个大规模开放视频数据集，包含13亿条视频URL和下载的8000万段视频（总时长1000万小时），覆盖视频、音频和图像三种模态，用于多模态预训练。通过内容感知场景检测提取片段并合成视频和音频字幕，训练模型在视频-文本和音频-文本基准上取得竞争力表现，同时探索视频帧作为图像-文本数据的替代来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态预训练数据集在视频模态上规模受限且开放程度不足，阻碍了视频-音频-图像多模态模型的规模化发展。作者旨在构建一个前所未有的超大规模开放视频数据集，为多模态学习社区提供更丰富的数据资源。

Method: 从CommonCrawl中收集13亿条平台特定视频URL，实际下载8000万段视频（总时长1000万小时）。采用内容感知的场景检测技术提取视频片段，并为其合成视频和音频字幕。此外，通过提取场景变化帧，探索将视频帧作为图像-文本数据的替代来源。

Result: 在该数据集上训练的模型在标准视频-文本和音频-文本基准上取得竞争力表现，且随训练或模型规模增大呈现一致的性能提升。基于视频帧训练的图像-文本检索模型也获得了强性能，且视频帧的视觉分布与标准网络图像语料库存在明显差异。

Conclusion: LAION-BVD将数据集开放给研究社区，以前所未有的规模显著扩展了多模态视频的开放访问，弥补了当前多模态预训练数据集在视频规模上的不足。

Abstract: We present LAION-BVD, a large-scale open video dataset for multimodal learning, which contains 1.3B platform-specific video URLs collected from CommonCrawl. From these, we download 80M videos with a total duration of 10 million hours. The dataset is designed for multimodal pre-training across the video, audio, and image modalities. Using content-aware scene detection, we extract clips for which we synthetically generate video and audio captions. Models trained on these data achieve competitive performance on standard video-text and audio-text benchmarks, with consistent improvements as training or model scale increases. Additionally, we explore video frames as an alternative source of image-text data by extracting scene-changing frames. These frames exhibit a visual distribution distinct from standard web image corpora, and models trained on this dataset achieve strong image-text retrieval performance. We release LAION-BVD to the research community. It significantly expands open access to multimodal videos at an unprecedented scale.

</details>


### [94] [LeFlow: Generative Latent Flow Planning for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24855)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Junbin Lu,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeFlow，将潜变量世界模型中的在线轨迹优化规划重构为可复用的潜轨迹生成。LeFlow利用校正流模型在潜动力学空间中直接生成当前嵌入与目标嵌入之间的未来潜路径，通过逆动力学解码器将潜状态转移转换为动作块，并由冻结的世界模型通过自回归rollout验证候选方案。在四个目标条件像素控制基准上，LeFlow以固定预算的rollout选择替代迭代动作空间优化，取得一致的成功率提升，同时将规划时间降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜变量世界模型在规划时仍依赖在线轨迹优化：对每个状态-目标对，从零开始运行迭代优化器搜索最优动作序列，将世界模型视为黑箱模拟器。这种做法的核心问题在于：(1)每次重新规划都要付出完整的迭代优化成本；(2)跨查询不复用规划经验。作者质疑：一旦潜变量世界模型学成，规划本身是否可以被摊销化（amortized）？

Method: LeFlow包含三个核心组件：(1) 整流流（rectified-flow）模型作为潜轨迹先验，在从世界模型获得的潜动力学空间中，直接生成当前嵌入到目标嵌入之间的未来潜路径；(2) 逆动力学解码器将潜状态转移（latent transitions）解码为动作块（action chunks）；(3) 冻结的世界模型通过自回归rollout对每个候选路径进行验证，采用固定预算的rollout选择机制挑选最优方案。

Result: 在四个主流的目标条件像素控制基准任务上，LeFlow用摊销式潜规划（amortized latent planning）加固定预算rollout选择替代了迭代式动作空间优化。实验显示LeFlow在成功率上获得一致的提升，同时规划时间降低一个数量级（order-of-magnitude reduction）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，潜变量世界模型不仅应支持预测功能，还应支持可复用的规划先验（reusable planning priors），从而为世界模型架构设计提供新的方向。

Abstract: Latent world models are inherently strong encoders that transform image pixel to latent embedding, yet existing world models still rely on online trajectory optimization for action planning: for every state-goal pair, an iterative optimizer is run from scratch to search for optimal action sequences, treating the world model as a black-box simulator. This approach pays the full iterative optimization cost anew at every replanning step and reuses no planning experience across queries. In this work, we ask whether planning itself can be amortized once a latent world model has been learned. We present LeFlow, which learns a reusable latent trajectory prior operating directly in the latent dynamics space from the world model. LeFlow recasts planning as conditional latent trajectory generation: a rectified-flow model imagines a future latent path between the current and goal embeddings, an inverse dynamics decoder turns latent transitions into action chunks, and the frozen world model verifies each candidate by autoregressive rollout. Across four major goal-conditioned pixel-control benchmarks, LeFlow replaces iterative action-space optimization with amortized latent planning and fixed-budget rollout selection, achieving consistent success-rate gains with an order-of-magnitude reduction in planning time. Our results argue that latent world models should support not only prediction but reusable planning priors. Our code is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/LeFlow.

</details>


### [95] [From Seeing to Acting: Smart Glasses as First-Person Intelligence Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24877)
*Jiangning Zhang,Haojun Chen,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次以统一框架系统综述智能眼镜平台，将其视为"第一人称智能"系统，围绕感知-状态-交互-行动的可持续闭环，提出硬件八轴能力、七项基础能力、L0-L5演进层级、九维部署框架与证据阶梯等系统化分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜正从捕获/显示配件演进为第一人称智能平台，但相关文献在设备、任务和基准上高度碎片化。核心挑战不在于模型能否独立完成识别、问答、记忆或行动，而在于完整系统能否维持可靠、时间有效、可纠正、可治理的感知-状态-交互-行动闭环。现有综述缺乏统一框架来比较和评估这些系统。

Method: 通过统一框架系统化研究智能眼镜：形式化第一人称数据流与受约束任务效用；沿八项可验证的硬件能力轴刻画设备；围绕七项相互依赖的基础能力组织文献；引入L0-L5框架覆盖采集、反应式感知、情境辅助、持久状态、受治理行动和具身耦合六个阶段；在九个应用场景中关联任务与数据集、系统、产品、利益相关者、失败后果和证据缺口；提出九维部署框架、声明条件化评估协议和从受控测量到纵向实地验证与审计的证据阶梯。

Result: 该综述提供了多层次的系统化分析框架，使智能眼镜更具可比性、可部署性和可复现评估性，同时弥补了文献碎片化的问题，并为可信第一人称智能绘制了路线图。

Conclusion: 智能眼镜的研究重点应从单点模型能力转向完整系统的可持续闭环。作者通过统一框架和分维度评估工具，推动该领域从碎片化走向系统化，最终目标是实现可信赖的第一人称智能。

Abstract: Smart glasses are evolving from capture and display accessories into first-person intelligence platforms that connect human perception, persistent context, and digital or physical action. Their on-body viewpoint aligns with the wearer's vision, audition, motion, and hand-object interaction, but must operate under tight energy, thermal, privacy, and feedback constraints. Despite rapid progress in augmented reality, egocentric vision, multimodal models, human-computer interaction, and embodied intelligence, the literature remains fragmented across devices, tasks, and benchmarks. \textit{The key challenge is not whether a model can recognize, answer, remember, or act in isolation, but whether a complete system can sustain a reliable, temporally valid, correctable, and governable perception-state-interaction-action loop.} This survey is \textit{the \textbf{first} to systematically study smart glasses through such a unified framework}. We formalize first-person data flow and constrained task utility, characterize devices along eight verifiable hardware capability axes, organize the literature around seven interdependent foundational capabilities, and introduce an L0-L5 framework spanning capture, reactive perception, contextual assistance, persistent state, governed action, and embodied coupling. Across nine application scenes, we connect tasks with datasets, systems, products, stakeholders, failure consequences, and evidence gaps. We further present a nine-dimensional deployment framework, a claim-conditioned evaluation protocol, and an evidence ladder from controlled measurement to longitudinal field validation and audit. Together, these elements make smart glasses more comparable, deployable, and reproducibly evaluated, while outlining a roadmap toward trustworthy first-person intelligence.

</details>


### [96] [Progressively Learning Heterogeneous Skills in a Unified Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23258)
*Yue-Yi Zhang,Ming Gong,Linpu He,Wei-Shi Zheng,Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HetSkills是一个在统一潜在空间中渐进学习异构技能的角色控制系统框架。该框架通过共享动作解码器和直觉蒸馏机制，实现从追踪、文本生成到运动补全和下游任务适配的多技能无缝整合，在多项任务上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于物理的角色控制方法通常针对单一任务或单一数据源设计，难以在不同技能（如运动追踪、文本到运动生成、运动补全）之间实现共享与整合。作者希望构建一个统一框架，使不同来源、不同监督形式和不同任务学到的技能能够在同一个潜在空间中协同工作。

Method: HetSkills的核心方法包括：(1) 将潜在空间视为共享可执行接口；(2) 首先学习追踪技能，建立运动控制基础并创建可复用的共享运动解码器；(3) 引入运动直觉蒸馏（motion intuition distillation）来防止文本到运动技能通过捷径路径学习而非真正理解语言语义；(4) 设计任务引导模块（task-guidance module），根据高层语言指令动态调整动作。

Result: 实验表明HetSkills在运动追踪、文本到运动生成、运动补全和下游任务适配等多项任务中均表现有效，即使在具有挑战性的条件下也取得了令人满意的成功率。

Conclusion: HetSkills证明了通过统一的潜在空间和渐进式技能学习策略，可以在单一框架下支持异构技能的无缝整合与持续扩展，同时保持自然运动质量和对长时间跨度任务的高度适应性。

Abstract: We propose HetSkills, a novel framework designed to progressively learn heterogeneous skills within a unified latent space for physics-based character control. The core idea is to treat this latent space as a shared executable interface, enabling seamless integration of skills learned from diverse data sources, supervision forms, and tasks. HetSkills begins by learning a tracking skill that establishes a strong foundation in motion control and creates a shared motion decoder, which can be reused across tasks without the need for retraining or separate controllers. To prevent the text-to-motion skill from exploiting shortcut pathways instead of learning language semantics, we introduce motion intuition distillation to ground text-to-motion generation in language semantics and a task-guidance module that dynamically adjusts actions based on high-level language instructions. This enables HetSkills to preserve natural motion while continuously expanding its skill repertoire, making it highly adaptable for long-horizon tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness in motion tracking, text-to-motion generation, motion completion, and downstream task adaptation, achieving impressive success rates even under challenging conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Taming Visual Neglect: A Variational Information Bottleneck Framework for Adaptive Attention in Multimodal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23570)
*Kaito Tanaka,Yuji Nishimura,Keisuke Matsuda,Aya Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VIB-ICL，一个基于信息瓶颈原理的信息论框架，用于解释多模态上下文学习（ICL）中视觉上下文何时及为何有效。通过引入交叉模态信息增益（CMIG）量化和推导泛化界，证明多模态ICL在视觉信息非冗余时优于纯文本ICL；同时证明视觉上下文被忽略是信息瓶颈最优解，并据此提出注意力重分配原理及VIB-ICL算法，在五个基准上实现最高4.7%准确率提升和35%示范数量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型展现强大的上下文学习能力，但视觉上下文何时及为何帮助多模态ICL仍未被充分理解。经验研究揭示了一个令人困惑的二分现象：模型有时有效利用视觉示范，却常常完全忽视它们。作者希望通过信息论框架统一解释这一矛盾。

Method: 基于信息瓶颈原理提出VIB-ICL框架。引入CMIG（交叉模态信息增益）量化视觉上下文提供的额外互信息；推导泛化界证明多模态ICL的超额风险受CMIG支配；证明视觉上下文忽视在信息冗余时是信息瓶颈最优解，得到闭式注意力重分配原理；通过变分界估计CMIG并动态重分配注意力，实例化为VIB-ICL算法。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上实现了最高4.7%的准确率提升和35%的所需示范数量减少，实验结果验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: VIB-ICL框架成功化解了多模态ICL中视觉上下文"有效利用"与"完全忽视"之间的理论矛盾，将所谓的"失败模式"重新解释为信息瓶颈最优策略，并为多模态上下文学习提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Large vision-language models exhibit strong in-context learning (ICL) capabilities, yet when and why visual context helps multimodal ICL remains poorly understood. Empirical studies show a puzzling dichotomy: models sometimes effectively leverage visual demonstrations, yet often neglect them entirely. We propose VIB-ICL, an information-theoretic framework that resolves this dichotomy through the Information Bottleneck principle. We introduce the Cross-Modal Information Gain (CMIG), which quantifies the additional mutual information that visual context provides about the target beyond textual context. We derive a generalization bound showing that multimodal ICL's excess risk over text-only ICL is governed by the CMIG, proving that multimodal ICL provably outperforms text-only ICL when visual information is non-redundant. We further prove that visual context neglect, often viewed as a failure mode, is the Information Bottleneck-optimal solution when visual information is redundant, yielding a closed-form Attention Reallocation Principle that prescribes how visual attention weights should be adaptively adjusted. We instantiate this principle in the VIB-ICL algorithm, which estimates CMIG via variational bounds and dynamically reallocates attention. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements of up to 4.7\% accuracy gains and 35\% reduction in required demonstrations, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [98] [From Triage to Discharge: A Survey of NLP Tasks, Methods, and Open Challenges in the Emergency Department](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23627)
*Dipankar Srirag,Aditya Joshi,Salil Kanhere,Padmanesan Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了46篇聚焦急诊科（ED）三个核心阶段（分诊、诊断、处置）的NLP研究论文，涵盖分诊分类、临床摘要、自动诊断、报告生成和出院文档等任务，并总结了建模范式、评估实践、新兴基准与共享任务，以及该领域面临的开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科在时间压力下运行，产生临床对话、分诊笔记和出院文档等多模态数据。虽然NLP（尤其是预训练Transformer和大语言模型）的进展为急诊护理中语言和时间密集型环节提供了新机遇，但现有综述要么覆盖整个医院工作流的NLP图谱，要么聚焦特定任务，缺乏针对急诊科全流程NLP研究的系统性梳理。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者系统分析了46篇论文，按急诊科三个护理阶段（分诊、诊断、处置）进行组织，覆盖分诊分类、临床摘要、自动诊断、报告生成和出院文档等任务，并考察了建模范式、评估实践以及新兴的基准测试和共享任务。

Result: 研究发现跨任务的共同趋势包括：从任务特定神经架构向预训练语言模型的范式转移、对交互式临床系统日益增长的兴趣、以及对临床接地评估（clinically grounded evaluation）的更多关注。同时指出开放挑战包括有限的泛化能力、嘈杂的临床输入和工作流约束。

Conclusion: 该综述为急诊科NLP研究提供了全面的结构化梳理，识别了关键趋势与未解决问题，为未来ED-NLP研究指明了方向，强调了临床落地评估和跨机构泛化的重要性。

Abstract: Emergency departments (EDs) operate under time pressure, generating multimodal data such as clinical conversations, triage notes, and discharge documents. Recent advances in natural language processing (NLP), particularly pretrained transformers and large language models, have created new opportunities to support language and time-intensive stages of emergency care. Yet existing surveys map clinical NLP across the broader hospital workflow or focus on specific tasks. This survey analyses 46 papers spanning the three phases of ED: triage, diagnosis, and disposition, covering tasks such as triage classification, clinical summarisation, automatic diagnosis, report generation, and discharge documentation. We examine modelling paradigms, evaluation practices, and emerging benchmarks and shared tasks. Across tasks, we identify common trends, including a shift from task-specific neural architectures to pretrained language models, growing interest in interactive clinical systems, and increasing attention to clinically grounded evaluation. Finally, we detail open challenges such as limited generalisability, noisy clinical inputs, and workflow constraints that inform future ED-NLP research.

</details>


### [99] [Contextual Embedding Evidence for Main--Light Verb Distinctions in Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23645)
*Farah Adeeba,Miriam Butt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用UrduBERT、DunbaaBERT和多语言BERT等情境化嵌入模型，在1,126个包含七个乌尔都语动词的自然句子中检验了Butt关于乌尔都语轻动词的理论。结果发现主用与轻用动词在表示上有显著分离，但同一词元的中心向量仍比其他配对更接近，且轻动词的动词身份在目标掩蔽后仍可恢复（UrduBERT准确率达0.866）。这些发现为重动词在保持词元与动词特定表示结构的同时系统性区别于主用动词提供了计算证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌尔都语轻动词承载图式化的事件结构意义，同时在词汇上与其对应的主动词保持关联。本研究旨在用计算语言学方法（情境化嵌入）检验Butt理论中关于轻动词与主动词表示关系的关键预测，即轻动词与主用之间存在系统性差异，同时又保留词元层面的词汇关联性。

Method: 研究采用三种预训练情境化语言模型（UrduBERT、DunbaaBERT、multilingual BERT），对包含七个乌尔都语动词的1,126个自然句子提取上下文嵌入。通过计算主用与轻用动词的表示分离度（21种动词-模型比较）、比较同词元主轻动词质心距离与异词元配对距离，并执行七分类预测任务（目标被掩蔽后预测动词身份），同时采用前置形式不相交（preceding-form-disjoint）评估以检验泛化能力。

Result: 所有21种动词-模型比较中，主用与轻用表示均呈现显著分离；同词元主轻动词质心始终比异词元配对更接近，支持词汇关联性保留。七分类预测中，UrduBERT在目标掩蔽后达到0.866准确率和0.852宏F1分数；在前置形式不相交评估下仍保持0.782准确率，表明模型能够超越对局部动词重复组合的记忆进行泛化。

Conclusion: 实验结果与Butt的理论一致：乌尔都语轻动词在表示上与主用动词系统性不同，同时保留词元特定和动词特定的表示结构。这为轻动词的双重属性（既独立于主用又与其保持词汇关联）提供了计算验证。

Abstract: Urdu light verbs contribute schematic event-structural meaning while remaining lexically related to corresponding main verbs. This study tests representational predictions derived from Butt's analysis using contextual embeddings from UrduBERT, DunbaaBERT, and multilingual BERT across 1,126 naturally occurring sentences containing seven Urdu verbs. Main and light uses show significant representational separation in all 21 verb--model comparisons. At the same time, same-lemma main and light centroids are consistently closer than mismatched main--light lemma pairs, supporting continued lexical relatedness. In a seven-way prediction task restricted to light uses, verb identity remains recoverable after the target is masked, with UrduBERT achieving 0.866 accuracy and 0.852 macro-F1. UrduBERT also retains 0.782 accuracy under a preceding-form-disjoint evaluation, indicating generalization beyond repeated local verb combinations. These findings provide computational evidence consistent with Butt's account that Urdu light verbs differ systematically from their main uses while retaining lemma-specific and verb-specific representational structure.

</details>


### [100] [The Limits of Automatic Evaluation of Creativity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23705)
*Alessandro Tutone,Giorgio Franceschelli,Mirco Musolesi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM生成文本的自动评估方法能否可靠地反映人类对创造力的判断。通过收集人类对WritingPrompts数据集中人类和AI生成短篇故事的11个创造力维度的评分，并与自动客观指标及LLM-as-a-Judge评估进行对比，发现自动评估与人类判断存在严重错位：LLM评委系统性地偏好AI生成的故事，而广泛使用的自动指标与人类判断的相关性接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成文本的能力日益增强，但衡量其创造力的自动化评估仍是一大挑战。现有研究缺乏对人类判断与自动评估方法之间一致性的系统验证，因此需要探讨当前自动评估方法能否可靠捕捉人类对创造力的判断。

Method: 论文收集了人类对WritingPrompts数据集中人类和AI生成的短篇故事的评分，涵盖11个创造力维度（如不可预测性、风格特征等）；随后将这些人类判断与自动化客观指标以及LLM-as-a-Judge评估结果进行比较，并通过相关性分析评估自动评估方法的一致性。

Result: 实验发现自动评估与人类评估之间存在显著错位。LLM评委表现出对AI生成故事的系统性偏好，倾向于偏爱其风格特征而低估人类文本的不可预测性等其他质量。此外，广泛使用的自动评估指标与人类判断的相关性接近零，表明它们无法捕捉创造力的关键维度。

Conclusion: 当前自动评估创造性的方法存在根本性局限，创造力的多维性和主观性难以被简化归结为计算指标。该研究强调了开发更可靠、更能与人类判断对齐的创造性文本评估方法的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of generating text that challenges human performance in domains requiring creativity, yet evaluating creativity in LLM-generated content remains a significant challenge. Here, we investigate whether current automatic evaluation methods can reliably capture human judgments of creativity. We collect human evaluations of human- and AI-generated short stories from the WritingPrompts dataset across 11 dimensions of creativity, and compare these judgments with automated objective metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments reveal substantial misalignment between automatic evaluations and human assessments. In particular, LLM-based judges exhibit a systematic preference for AI-generated stories, consistently favoring their stylistic characteristics over the unpredictability and other qualities of human-authored texts. Furthermore, correlation analyses show that widely used automatic metrics exhibit near-zero alignment with human judgments across both human- and AI-generated stories, suggesting that they fail to capture important dimensions of creativity. These findings highlight fundamental limitations in current approaches to the automatic evaluation of creative text and underscore the difficulty of reducing the multidimensional and subjective nature of creativity to computational metrics.

</details>


### [101] [ADE: Agentic Data Evolution Framework for Human-Centered Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23719)
*Yang Yu,Yilin Jiang,Zexuan Fei,Yiming Luo,Xingkai Song,Kaiyi Huang,Aimin Zhou,Xin Lin,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Data Evolution (ADE)，一种数据为中心的框架，通过闭环的Observation-Variation-Selection (OVS)流程组织合成监督数据，并以稳态准入机制充当质量棘轮，保守地门控更新以实现持续跨轮改进。在DEV300上，内部胜率从50%提升至75.81%，外部胜率从55.20%提升至68.86%，并在多种后训练方法、模型规模和任务上均表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
