Result: 心理测量验证表明，LLM驱动的相似度指标与构念无关的局部依赖（construct-irrelevant local dependence）指标更为吻合，且比传统文本度量产生更一致的题目参数分组。CAT模拟表明，将LLM相似度约束纳入题目选择可提高估计稳定性并降低偏差，且效率损失很小，优于基于传统指标的约束。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LLM驱动的AISA在支持可扩展题库管理（bank curation）、内容感知的测试组卷（content-aware test assembly）以及体验敏感的适应性测试（experience-sensitive adaptive testing）方面具有巨大潜力，可广泛应用于多样化的测评场景。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of large-scale assessments and the growing adoption of automatic item generation have intensified concerns about incidental content redundancy, where construct-irrelevant elements such as wording or contextual framing become unintentionally repetitive across items. Traditional similarity metrics like BLEU or cosine similarity, often fail to capture the nuanced structural and semantic layers that drive perceived redundancy simultaneously. This study proposes a dual-dimensional framework for Automated Item Similarity Analysis (AISA) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), operationalizing similarity through Structured Decomposition and Semantic Relatedness. Psychometric validation indicates that LLM-derived metrics align more closely with indicators of construct-irrelevant local dependence and yield more coherent item parameter groupings than traditional text-based measures. The framework is further evaluated through its application in Computerized Adaptive Testing (CAT). Simulations reveal that incorporating LLM-based similarity constraints into item selection improves estimation stability and reduces bias with minimal efficiency trade-offs, outperforming constraints based on conventional metrics. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-powered AISA to support scalable bank curation, content-aware test assembly, and experience-sensitive adaptive testing across diverse assessment contexts.

</details>


### [265] [FedV-KGQA: Multi-Hop Question Answering over Vertically Partitioned Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24846)
*Md Saikat Islam Khan Bappy,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FedV-KGQA框架，用于解决垂直划分（各组织共享实体但拥有不相交的关系集合）的多源知识图谱多跳问答问题。该框架通过本地图增强和知识图谱嵌入相结合，确保原始三元组和关系参数不出本地，无需运行时跨组织通信即可实现多跳推理，并在12种模型配置、三个基准测试上表现接近集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的知识图谱问答数据常因治理和数据主权约束而分布在不同的组织中。集中式系统无法回答所需事实被拆分在垂直划分的数据孤岛中的多跳问题，因此需要一种能在不共享原始数据的前提下实现跨组织多跳推理的联邦式框架。

Method: 提出FedV-KGQA框架，核心包含两个机制：(1) 本地图增强与知识图谱嵌入相结合，确保原始三元组和关系参数永远不会离开各数据孤岛，建立结构化的数据边界而无需集中式图访问；(2) 引入主题实体锚定机制，在无需运行时跨孤岛通信的情况下将问题锚定到正确的图邻域。

Result: 在三个基准测试上评估了12种模型配置，结果显示FedV-KGQA性能强劲，接近集中式方法的性能水平，能够泛化到3跳推理，并且对嵌入扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedV-KGQA证明了在不共享原始数据、无需中央图访问的前提下，联邦垂直划分的知识图谱多跳问答是可行的，且性能与集中式方法接近，具备良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world data for knowledge graph question answering is often distributed across different organizations due to governance and data sovereignty constraints. While centralized systems exist, they cannot answer multi-hop questions when the required facts are split across vertically partitioned silos. In this paper, we propose FedV-KGQA, a framework for multi-hop reasoning over knowledge graphs in which organizations share entities but own disjoint sets of relations. Our approach combines local graph enrichment and knowledge graph embeddings to ensure raw triples and relation parameters never leave each silo, establishing a structural data boundary without requiring centralized graph access. We further introduce a topic entity anchoring mechanism that grounds questions in the correct graph neighborhood without any runtime inter-silo communication. We evaluate 12 model configurations across three benchmarks and show that FedV-KGQA performs strongly, remains close to centralized performance, generalizes to 3-hop reasoning, and is robust to embedding perturbations.

</details>


### [266] [SPO++: Stream-Aligned Policy Optimization for Asynchronous Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24870)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Qianshan Wei,Ziqi Zhou,Zihe Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPO++通过修正优势标准化中的度量不匹配问题（用动作-token度量代替轨迹度量）并提出按策略事件而非接收顺序组织提示证据，在ALFWorld和Math-TIR任务上相比SPO提升了在线学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习（Group-relative RL）需要等待同一提示的兄弟rollout，对于长且可变的工具使用轨迹成本高昂。SPO通过持续提示级价值估计解除了此依赖，但其将每个轨迹的优势白化（whitening）后再优化token平均actor损失的做法存在缺陷：轨迹级集中化一般不使actor实际消费的token加权量居中，导致偏差。

Method: 1) 在动作-token度量下标准化终局结果优势，修复轨迹中心化与token加权数量之间的不匹配；2) 按策略事件（生成证据的策略）而非学习者接收顺序来组织提示证据。

Result: 在ALFWorld两种模型规模及Math-TIR上的匹配运行中，SPO++相比SPO提升了在线学习效率。配对消融实验表明动作-token度量标准化是最强测试组件。

Conclusion: SPO++通过优势标准化与证据组织的改进，有效解决了SPO中轨迹级标准化与token级目标之间的度量失配问题，进一步提升了单流策略优化的学习效率。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning waits for sibling rollouts of the same prompt, which is costly for long and variable tool-use trajectories. Single-stream Policy Optimization (SPO) removes this dependency with a persistent prompt-level value estimate, but its recipe whitens one advantage per trajectory before optimizing a token-mean actor loss. We show that trajectory centering generally does not center the token-weighted quantity consumed by the actor, and fix the mismatch by standardizing terminal-outcome advantages under the action-token measure. We additionally organize prompt evidence by the policy event that generated it rather than learner receipt order. Across matched runs on ALFWorld at two model scales and on Math-TIR, SPO++ improves online learning efficiency over SPO. A paired ablation identifies action-token-measure normalization as the strongest tested component.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Orientation in Extended Position-Based Dynamics: Application to Rigid Bodies and Cosserat Rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23606)
*Samuel Tobin,Caleb Rucker*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文展示了李群理论为扩展位置动力学（XPBD）中旋转自由度提供一个统一框架，涵盖旋转表达、约束、插值和微分，从而改进刚体约束（动态一致性提升超过10^4倍）以及高阶有限元Cosserat杆模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XPBD中处理3D旋转自由度需要在非线性流形上进行计算，传统方法缺乏统一且干净的理论框架，导致刚体约束的动态一致性和Cosserat杆离散的精度受限。

Method: 利用李群理论统一表达XPBD中的旋转、约束、插值和微分；推导出显式的李理论约束公式及其梯度用于刚体模拟；通过流形上的插值将框架扩展至高阶有限元Cosserat杆。

Result: 约束刚体模拟的动态一致性较最先进方法提升超过10^4倍；线性有限元优于传统链式刚体离散；高阶基函数提供更平滑的解决方案和更快的收敛速度；在大变形和接触场景中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 李群理论为XPBD中的旋转自由度提供了一个统一且高效的框架，同时改善了刚体约束的物理一致性和Cosserat杆的数值精度，在高阶基函数下具有更好的收敛表现。

Abstract: Rotational degrees of freedom in Extended Position-Based Dynamics (XPBD) require computations on the nonlinear manifold of 3D rotations. We show that Lie theory provides a clean, unified framework for expressing rotations, constraints, interpolation, and differentiation in XPBD, enabling both improved rigid-body constraints and higher-order finite-element Cosserat rods. We derive explicit Lie-theoretic constraint formulations and their gradients for rigid-body simulation, improving the dynamic consistency of constrained rigid-body simulations in XPBD by a factor of over $10^4$ compared to the state-of-the-art. Our framework naturally extends to finite-element Cosserat rods by enabling on-manifold interpolation of nodal rotations. Linear finite elements outperform the conventional chain-of-rigid-bodies discretization, while higher-order basis functions provide even smoother solutions and faster convergence. Utility is demonstrated in a variety of examples with large deformations and contact.

</details>


### [268] [A Three-Parameter Binary Subdivision Scheme for Shape-Controlled Curve Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23637)
*Rabia Hameed,Jihad Younis,Maryam Salem Alatawi,Tahreem Farman,Hafiza Sana Mukhtiar*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型三参数9点二进制逼近细分方案，通过7点Lagrange和7点B样条细分方案的加权组合构造，利用设计控制参数调节极限曲线的几何特性，并建立了支撑、连续性、端点规则和Gibbs振荡等理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算机辅助几何设计、计算机图形学和工程应用中，形状可控的曲线设计具有基础性作用。现有细分方案往往缺乏灵活的参数化调节能力，难以在逼近精度和几何形状控制之间取得平衡，因此需要构造一种具有可调几何特性且保持逼近性质的统一细分框架。

Method: 通过加权组合7点Lagrange和7点B样条细分方案的细化规则，利用两个父方案对应细化点之间的位移向量，结合相邻位移向量通过三个独立的设计控制参数构造合成向量，进而推导细化规则，生成统一的二进制细分方案族。通过施加约束得到两个代表性子方案；理论分析涵盖支撑、连续性、开多边形端点规则及Gibbs振荡行为。

Result: 建立了所提细分方案族的支撑区、连续性阶、端点规则和Gibbs振荡行为的理论结果，并通过图形示例展示了三个设计控制参数对极限曲线形状的调节作用。两个代表性子方案作为特殊情况被推导出来并验证。

Conclusion: 该细分框架凭借灵活的几何构造、直观的参数化和良好的数学性质，为设计控制的曲线造型提供了灵活有效的工具，在计算机辅助几何设计及相关工程应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Shape-controlled curve design plays a fundamental role in computer-aided geometric design, computer graphics, and engineering applications. In this paper, we present a novel three-parameter 9-point binary approximating subdivision scheme constructed by a weighted combination of the refinement rules of the 7-point Lagrange and 7-point B-spline subdivision schemes. The proposed construction employs displacement vectors between the corresponding refinement points of the parent schemes and constructs resultant vectors by combining neighbouring displacement vectors through three independent design-control parameters. These resultant vectors are subsequently used to derive the refinement rules, thereby yielding a unified family of binary subdivision schemes with adjustable geometric characteristics while preserving the approximating nature of the refinement process. Two representative sub-schemes are derived as special cases by imposing suitable constraints on the design-control parameters. Theoretical investigations establish the support, continuity, endpoint rules for open polygons, and Gibbs oscillation behavior of the proposed family, while graphical examples demonstrate the influence of the design-control parameters on the generated limit curves. Owing to its flexible geometric construction, intuitive parameterization, and favorable mathematical properties, the proposed subdivision framework provides a flexible and effective framework for design-controlled curve design and a valuable tool for computer-aided geometric design and related engineering applications.

</details>


### [269] [ExMesh++: From Multi-View Images to Relightable UV-PBR Mesh Assets via Topology-Adaptive Reconstruction and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24109)
*Chuanjin Fan,Lifan Wu,Wenjie Chang,Hanzhi Chang,Wenfei Yang,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ExMesh++ 是一个分阶段框架，用于从多视角图像重建可重光照的 UV-PBR 网格资产，通过先精化显式网格几何与拓扑，再固定网格-UV载体并优化UV空间PBR贴图与环境光照，最终产出可直接用于标准DCC工作流的资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表面重建方法（隐式场、高斯原语等中间表示）需要表面提取和纹理烘焙才能转为可用资产，而逆渲染方法将材质和光照绑定在神经场或点云原语上而非最终网格上。此外，几何、材质、光照联合优化时变量间可能相互补偿，导致分解存在歧义。这些局限阻碍了高质量、可编辑、可重光照的网格资产的直接产出。

Method: ExMesh++ 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过自适应顶点分裂与合并精化显式网格几何和拓扑，同时保持拓扑变化过程中的UV一致性；第二阶段固定网格-UV载体，联合优化UV空间PBR材质贴图与环境光照，并利用次级光线追踪结合共享UV-PBR材质来建模单次弹射的漫反射间接光照。

Result: 实验表明该方法在几何精度上具有竞争力，重光照性能强，导出的资产可直接用于标准DCC（数字内容创作）工作流。

Conclusion: 通过将几何/拓扑精化与材质/光照优化解耦为两个稳定阶段，并将间接光照建模在固定的UV-PBR载体上，ExMesh++有效避免了联合优化中的歧义分解问题，实现了可直接使用的可重光照PBR网格资产重建。

Abstract: Multi-view reconstruction extends beyond surface recovery to editable and relightable mesh assets. Such assets require well-formed topology, valid UV parameterization, and explicit PBR material maps. Existing surface reconstruction approaches optimize implicit fields, Gaussian primitives, or other intermediate representations. Converting them into such assets often requires surface extraction and texture baking. Inverse-rendering methods estimate materials and illumination, yet these components often remain tied to neural fields or point-based primitives rather than the final mesh. Joint optimization of geometry, materials, and lighting may also allow these variables to compensate for one another, leading to ambiguous decomposition. To address these limitations, we present ExMesh++, a staged framework for reconstructing relightable UV-PBR mesh assets from multi-view images. The first stage refines explicit mesh geometry and topology through adaptive vertex splitting and merging, while maintaining UV consistency as the topology changes. The second stage fixes the resulting mesh-UV carrier and optimizes UV-space PBR maps together with environment lighting. Building on this stable carrier, ExMesh++ models one-bounce diffuse indirect illumination through secondary-ray tracing with shared UV-PBR materials. Experiments demonstrate competitive geometry accuracy, strong relighting performance, and direct usability of the exported assets in standard DCC workflows.

</details>
